> Itọkasi pipe fun gbogbo oniyipada ayika ti a mọ nipasẹ OmniRoute. Fun awoṣe ibẹrẹ ni iyara, wo [`.env.example`](../../.env.example).

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Gbogbo oniyipada ti o ni akọsilẹ nibi gbọdọ tun han ni `.env.example`, ati gbogbo oniyipada ni `.env.example` gbọdọ han nibi. `npm run check:env-doc-sync` fi ipa mu eyi lori ṣiṣe ati ni CI. Lati fi oniyipada kan silẹ ni idi, ṣafikun si atokọ iyọọda inu `scripts/check/check-env-doc-sync.mjs`.

## 1. Asiri ti a beere

Aṣiri aṣiri ti samisi** Bẹẹni** jẹ ipilẹṣẹ laifọwọyi lori ṣiṣe akọkọ (`JWT_SECRET`, `API_KEY_SECRET`) ati tẹsiwaju si `server.env` ninu iwe ilana data — ṣeto wọn ni gbangba nigbati itọsọna yẹn jẹ ephemeral. Iyatọ jẹ `OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET`: ko tẹsiwaju lati `server.env` — awọn aaye titẹsi olupin mint iye ephemeral fun bata bata nikan ni afara ilana le lo, nitorinaa awọn alabara afara ita nilo lati ṣeto ni gbangba.

| Ayípadà                      | Ti beere fun         | Aiyipada                | Faili orisun                                              | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ---------------------------- | -------------------- | ----------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `JWT_SECRET`                 | **Bẹẹni**            | _(ko si)_               | `src/lib/auth`                                            | Signs/verifies gbogbo kuki igba dasibodu (JWT). Ṣe ina pẹlu `openssl rand -base64 48`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `API_KEY_SECRET`             | **Bẹẹni**            | _(ko si)_               | `src/lib/db/apiKeys.ts`                                   | Bọtini fifi ẹnọ kọ nkan AES fun awọn iye bọtini API ni isinmi ni SQLite. Ṣẹda pẹlu `openssl rand -hex 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `INITIAL_PASSWORD`           | Rara                 | _(a ko ṣeto)_           | `src/lib/auth/managementPassword.ts`                      | Ṣeto ọrọ igbaniwọle dasibodu ni ibẹrẹ lakoko ti ko si ọkan ti o fipamọ sibẹsibẹ; bikita ni kete ti ọrọ igbaniwọle ti wa ni fipamọ. npm ati orisun fi sori ẹrọ daakọ `.env.example` si `.env`, nitorinaa wọn bẹrẹ pẹlu aaye ti o mọ ni gbangba `CHANGEME`: rọpo rẹ ṣaaju bata akọkọ, tabi ṣeto `INITIAL_PASSWORD=` (ofo) ni `.env` yẹn lati ṣẹda ọrọ igbaniwọle ni oluṣeto lori ọkọ ni ibẹwo akọkọ, gẹgẹbi iye aito (aiyipada aworan Docker ) ṣe. Yi ọrọ igbaniwọle ti o fipamọ pada ni Dashboard → Eto → Aabo, tabi ṣiṣe `omniroute reset-password` (`node bin/reset-password.mjs` ni ibi isanwo orisun) pẹlu `DATA_DIR` ṣeto si itọsọna data olupin naa. |
| `OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET` | **Bẹẹni** (igbejade) | _(a ko ṣeto)_           | `src/app/api/internal/codex-responses-ws/route.ts`        | Pipin ikoko fun awọn ti abẹnu Codex Idahun WebSocket Afara. Jẹri awọn ibeere afara laarin Electron/browser WS relay ati OmniRoute. ⚠️**REQUIRED ni gbóògì — nigba ti unset, gbogbo WS ibeere afara ti wa ni kọ.** Ṣe ina pẹlu `openssl rand -base64 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_SW_BUILD_ID`      | Rara                 | _(git SHA) _            | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/assembleStandalone.mjs` | Id kaṣe-busting ti oṣiṣẹ ti o han gbangba fun ikarahun PWA (# 11779); akọkọ ni pq o ga.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `SOURCE_VERSION`             | Rara                 | _(a ko ṣeto)_           | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/assembleStandalone.mjs` | Keji ni pq — ṣeto nipasẹ PaaS ọmọle (eg Heroku-ara) bi awọn ransogun dá.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_SW_BUILD_ID`    | Rara                 | _(ti ari)_              | `src/shared/components/PwaRegister.tsx`                   | Kọ-akoko àkọsílẹ iye ti ose nlo lati forukọsilẹ `/sw.js?v=…`; ti o wa lati awọn meji loke, lẹhinna git SHA.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_STAMP_TOKEN` | Rara (laifọwọyi)     | _(laifọwọyi fun bata) _ | `src/server/authz/policies/management.ts`                 | Aṣiri-ilana ti n ṣe afihan ontẹ ẹlẹgbẹ-IP ti o gbẹkẹle wa lati olupin HTTP ti OmniRoute ti ara rẹ (`scripts/dev/peer-stamp.mjs`). Awọn igbẹkẹle authz middleware beere agbegbe (loopback/LAN gating ti awọn ipa ọna LOCAL_ONLY ) nikan nigbati ontẹ ba gbe ami yii. Ipilẹṣẹ laifọwọyi bata — kọọkan ti a ko ṣeto; nikan PIN o fun olona-ilana setups ti o gbọdọ pin ontẹ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRUSTED_PROXIES`  | Rara                 | _(a ko ṣeto)_           | `scripts/dev/peer-stamp.mjs`                              | IPs ti o ya sọtọ Comma tabi awọn sakani CIDR ti awọn aṣoju iyipada ti `X-Forwarded-For` / `X-Real-IP` atokọ IP allow/deny le gbagbọ, lori oke loopback, awọn adirẹsi nẹtiwọki aladani ati awọn egbegbe Cloudflare, eyiti o jẹ igbẹkẹle nigbagbogbo. Nilo nikan fun aṣoju lori eyikeyi adirẹsi gbogbo eniyan miiran; bibẹkọ ti awọn aṣoju ile ti ara adirẹsi ni ohun ti àlẹmọ awọn onidajọ. Awọn bọtini itọsi fun-onibara (`/api/v1/relay/chat/completions`) yii lori adirẹsi alabara ti o yanju kanna, rara lori gbigbe awọn akọle siwaju nikan.                                                                                                          |

### Awọn aṣẹ iran

```bash
# Generate all four secrets at once:
echo "JWT_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 48)"
echo "API_KEY_SECRET=$(openssl rand -hex 32)"
echo "INITIAL_PASSWORD=$(openssl rand -base64 16)"
echo "OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 32)"
```

> [!CAUTION]
> Maṣe ṣe awọn faili `.env` pẹlu awọn aṣiri gidi si iṣakoso ẹya. `.gitignore` ti yọkuro `.env` tẹlẹ, ṣugbọn jẹri ṣaaju titari.

## 2. Ibi ipamọ & aaye data

OmniRoute**SQLite** (nipasẹ `better-sqlite3`) fun gbogbo itẹramọṣẹ. Awọn oniyipada wọnyi ṣakoso ipo data, fifi ẹnọ kọ nkan, ati igbesi aye.

| Ayípadà                                    | Aiyipada                      | Faili orisun                                          | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `DATA_DIR`                                 | `~/.omniroute/`               | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Atọka gbongbo fun SQLite DB, awọn afẹyinti, ati awọn faili data. Daju fun awọn iwọn Docker tabi awọn ọna aṣa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_DEFAULT_DATA_DIR`         | _(a ko ṣeto)_                 | `src/lib/dataPaths.ts`                                | Sa niyeon fun test/eval DATA_DIR oluso (# 10428). Idanwo ati Node eval/print probes (`-e`/`--eval`/`-p`/`--print`, pẹlu `--eval=`/`--print=` fọọmu) pẹlu ko si `DATA_DIR` ti wa ni darí si a throwaway temp dir ki nwọn ko ba le ṣi awọn oniṣẹ gidi database; ṣeto si `1` lati jade pada si itọsọna gidi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILD_SHA`                      | _(a ko ṣeto)_                 | `src/lib/monitoring/buildSha.ts`                      | Git SHA ti artifact nṣiṣẹ. Ontẹ nipasẹ `npm run build:release`; injectable ninu awọn apoti ti o gbe lai si `dist/BUILD_SHA` sentinel. Dada bi `system.buildSha` lori `/api/monitoring/health`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_RELEASE_REF`                    | `origin/main`                 | `scripts/build/buildProvenance.ts`                    | Ref pack-artifact provenance ẹnu-ọna sọwedowo Kọ SHA lodi si (# 10427).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_CANARY_BUILD`             | _(a ko ṣeto)_                 | `scripts/build/buildProvenance.ts`                    | Ṣeto si `1` lati gba iṣakojọpọ kikọ ti SHA ko si lori laini itusilẹ, ṣe igbasilẹ rẹ bi canary ti o mọọmọ dipo kiko ẹnu-bode (#10427).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_API_KEY`                  | _(a ko ṣeto)_                 | `scripts/ops/deploy-canary.mjs`                       | bọtini API fun canary-fi mu ẹfin ibere, rán bi `Authorization: Bearer` lori `/v1/chat/completions`. Nikan lo nipasẹ iwe afọwọkọ imuṣiṣẹ (#10429), kii ṣe nipasẹ olupin. Ko ni ibatan si awọn oniyipada `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_*` ti ijade-inu CLI ẹfin ẹfin (`RUN_CLI_SMOKE=1`, `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_BASE_URL/MODEL/API_KEY_ENV/TARGETS/TIMEOUT_MS` ni `tests/integration/upstream-cli-smoke.int.test.ts`) — wo [CLI Integrations → èéfín gidi](../guides/CLI-INTEGRATIONS.md).                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILDING`                       | _(a ko ṣeto)_                 | `src/lib/buildPhase.ts`                               | Kọ-alakoso ifihan agbara (# 10060): ṣeto si `1` nipa `scripts/build/build-next-isolated.mjs` ati ki o jogun nipa gbogbo spawned Kọ Osise ki awọn DB Layer pada a ko si-op stub dipo ti ikojọpọ awọn abinibi better-sqlite3 Addoni (eyi ti aborts osise lori ijade). Maṣe ṣeto fun olupin ti nṣiṣẹ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_NATIVE_DEP_CHECK`          | `0`                           | `scripts/check/check-native-deps.mjs`                 | Ṣeto si `1` lati fo iṣayẹwo iṣaju-igbẹkẹle abinibi yiyan fun awọn igi ti o taja nla. Eleyi ko ni ṣe a sonu gbára buildable; lo nikan nigbati awọn igbẹkẹle abinibi ti pese ni ita ẹgbẹ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_DATA_DIR`                       | _(a ko ṣeto)_                 | `open-sse/executors/promptql/threadSticky.ts`         | **Fallback inagijẹ** fun `DATA_DIR`, ṣayẹwo nikan nigbati `DATA_DIR` ko ṣeto. Ti a lo lati wa ibi-igbimọ PromptQL lori disiki o tẹle-alalepo igba kaṣe (`<dir>/promptql-thread-sessions.json`); ti ko ba ṣeto var, kaṣe naa wa ni iranti nikan (ko duro kọja awọn atunbere).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLUGINS_DIR`                    | _(a ko ṣeto)_                 | `src/lib/plugins/scanner.ts`                          | Directory awọn** asiko isise itanna** ka — ati gbongbo oluṣakoso ohun itanna nfi sori ẹrọ si — ti o bori aiyipada ti arijade (#11827). Tọkasi si igi itanna ti a fi dipọ ni Docker/K8s dipo gbigbe HOME lati tun gbe ọna ọlọjẹ (HOME ṣe akoso gbogbo ihuwasi ibatan ile miiran paapaa). Unset = `~/.omniroute/plugins`, tabi `/tmp/.omniroute/plugins` nigbati ilana naa ko ṣe okeere si ile ni gbogbo — ti kii ṣe awari ipalọlọ yi oniyipada yọkuro. Ilana ti o yanju jẹ ibuwolu wọle lẹẹkan ni ibẹrẹ bi `scanner.dir_resolved` pẹlu titẹ sii ti o bori. Ẹgbẹ olupin nikan: awọn afikun aṣẹ CLI tọju `OMNIROUTE_PLUGIN_PATH` tiwọn (apakan 9). |
| `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY`                   | _(o ṣofo = alaabo)_           | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | bọtini AES fun fifi ẹnọ kọ nkan SQLite ni kikun ni isinmi. Ṣe ina pẹlu `openssl rand -hex 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY_VERSION`           | `v1`                          | `scripts/build/bootstrap-env.mjs`, `electron/main.js` | Aami ẹya fun bọtini fifi ẹnọ kọ nkan. Ilọsi nigbati o ba n ṣiṣẹ yiyi bọtini lati ṣe atilẹyin idinku awọn afẹyinti atijọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP`               | `false`                       | `src/lib/db/backup.ts`                                | Nigba ti `true`, foo routine/pre-write SQLite faili awọn afẹyinti (models.dev pricing save/clear, awọn eto kọ). Afọwọṣe ati awọn iṣaju-pada sipo awọn afẹyinti ṣi ṣiṣẹ. O ṣe** kii ṣe** mu aworan ifaworanhan ailewu ti o tọ ti olusare iṣiwa ti o jẹ dandan tabi oluso ijira pupọ fun DB ti o tẹpẹlẹ tẹlẹ. Awọn afẹyinti ti kii ṣe afọwọṣe ni a fa si ni pupọ julọ lẹẹkan fun iṣẹju 60. Dasibodu** Eto → Ibi ipamọ** le mu afẹyinti aifọwọyi ṣiṣẹ ni ominira.                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_SQLITE_SLOW_MS`                 | _(unset/off)_                 | `src/lib/db/adapters/slowDiagnostics.ts`              | Miliseconds; iye ni isalẹ 1 tabi ti kii-nọmba duro pa; kilo ni pupọ julọ lẹẹkan fun 10s; laini log ni awakọ, iṣiṣẹ, ati elapsedMs nikan.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_SQLITE_OPTIMIZE_ENABLED`        | `true`                        | `src/lib/db/sqlitePlannerStats.ts`                    | Itọju awọn iṣiro oluṣeto abẹlẹ, ominira ti imuduro imuduro: iwe-itọju afọmọ akọkọ ṣiṣẹ (deede 30s lẹhin ibẹrẹ, lẹhin awọn iṣiwa) nṣiṣẹ `PRAGMA optimize=0x10002`; awọn igbasilẹ ti o tẹle ni gbogbo wakati 6 ṣiṣe `PRAGMA optimize` (tabi `PRAGMA optimize=0x00002` fun itupalẹ ailopin). `false` tabi `0` ṣe alaabo gbogbo itọju (aibikita ọran, gige gige funfun). Awọn apoti isura infomesonu iranti ati awọn ọna afẹyinti kika-nikan ko ni ipa. Awọn ikuna ti wa ni ibuwolu wọle, kii ṣe ju silẹ.                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_SQLITE_ANALYSIS_LIMIT`          | `400`                         | `src/lib/db/sqlitePlannerStats.ts`                    | Ṣeto `PRAGMA analysis_limit` ṣaaju iṣapeye. Awọn odidi eleemewa ni a dimọ si [0, 100000]; awọn iye malformed/empty aiyipada si 400. `0` awọn ibeere ailopin, itupalẹ gangan ti awọn tabili ti a yan nipasẹ iṣapeye; Pass periodic clears SQLite's iṣapeye iṣapẹẹrẹ-fila bit lati bu ọla fun eyi. Ko si adaduro gbólóhùn onínọmbà SQL ti wa ni ṣiṣe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_CRYPT_KEY`                      | _(a ko ṣeto)_                 | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | **Legacy inagijẹ** fun `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY`. Ti gba bi ipadasẹhin nigbati oniyipada akọkọ ko si.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_BASE64`                 | _(a ko ṣeto)_                 | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | **Legacy inagijẹ** (Base64-encoded fọọmu) gba bi a fallback. Yiyipada laifọwọyi ṣaaju lilo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_DB_HEALTHCHECK_INTERVAL_MS`     | _(a ko ṣeto)_                 | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Pa aarin igba ayẹwo ilera SQLite kuro (ms). Nigba ti a ba ti ṣeto, awọn aiyipada yoo wa lati `NODE_ENV`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_TRUNCATE_INTERVAL_MS`       | _(yiyọ)_                      | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | **Yiyọ kuro.** `wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)` ifiwe igbakọọkan le sọ maapu pinpin wal-index jẹ ki o ja ilana naa jẹ pẹlu SIGBUS (#13973), nitorinaa oluṣeto ko si mọ. Oniyipada naa jẹ inert: iye to daadaa ṣe igbasilẹ ikilọ idinku akoko-ọkan, lakoko ti `0` tabi aito yoo dakẹ. WAL naa jẹ itọju nipasẹ awọn aaye ayẹwo PASSIVE (isalẹ) ati gedu nipasẹ ibi ayẹwo titiipa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_PASSIVE_INTERVAL_MS`        | `300000` (5m)                 | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | Yipada si aarin `wal_checkpoint(PASSIVE)` loorekoore (ms). Ṣe idaduro awọn fireemu WAL kekere nitoribẹẹ awọn aaye ayẹwo wa ni iyara ati pe faili WAL naa duro ni didi laarin awọn ege tiipa. `0` mu ṣiṣẹ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_GUARD_MAX_MB`               | `256`                         | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | Nigbati ami PASSIVE kan ba ri faili WAL loke iwọn yii, ṣiṣe `wal_checkpoint(RESTART)` ki WAL bẹrẹ lai ṣe atunṣe wal-index ti ya aworan. Awọn aaye ayẹwo ipo truncate laaye ni a yọkuro (wo ila `OMNIROUTE_WAL_TRUNCATE_INTERVAL_MS` ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART`          | `false`                       | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressure.ts`                  | Ṣeto si `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` lati jade ilana naa lẹhin titẹ awọn orisun to ṣe pataki ti wa ni idaduro fun `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART_AFTER_MS`, jẹ ki olubẹwo kan (eto `Restart=always` ti eto, Docker tun bẹrẹ eto imulo) mu ilana ti o mọ pada pada dipo ṣiṣe iranṣẹ 503s titilai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART_AFTER_MS` | `120000` (2m)                 | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressure.ts`                  | Bawo ni titẹ pataki to ṣe pataki yẹ ki o tẹsiwaju ṣaaju ki ijade ti ara ẹni bẹrẹ ina.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_PSI_DISABLED`          | `false`                       | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressurePolicy.ts`            | Ṣeto si `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` lati foju parẹ-ogun `/proc/pressure/memory` (`psi_some` / `psi_full`) nigbati aladuugbo alariwo ba rin irin-ajo 503s lakoko ti ilana yii dara (#13124). Ẹgbẹ ati awọn oluso òkiti V8 duro lori. PSI tun dẹkun kika bi ẹri kernel throttle fun `cgroup_high`: apapọ akojọpọ kan loke `memory.high` lẹhinna yipada ni pataki nikan lori ilosoke `memory.events` `high`/`max`. Laisi `memory.events` ipin aise ti wa ni ipamọ.                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_SQLJS_WASM_PATH`                | _(Ṣawari-laifọwọyi)_          | `src/lib/db/adapters/sqljsAdapter.ts`                 | Ona ti o han gbangba (pipe tabi ojulumo si cwd) si `sql-wasm.wasm` nigba lilo `sql.js` WASM fallback ohun ti nmu badọgba. Awari-laifọwọyi nipasẹ awọn igbẹkẹle package ati awọn ipalemo oludije nigbati aito.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_BATCH_RETENTION_DAYS`           | `30`                          | `src/lib/db/cleanup.ts`                               | Awọn ọjọ ebute kan (completed/failed/cancelled/expired) Batch API awọn aaye ayẹwo iṣẹ, awọn faili input/output/error ti a tọka, ati ila ni a tọju nipasẹ gbigba afọmọ aifọwọyi ṣaaju piparẹ. Nikan gba ipa ni kete ti `BATCH_AND_FILE_AUTO_CLEANUP_ENABLED` ti wa ni titan; awọn ibaamu OpenAI ti ara Batch API window idaduro igbejade. Ko ni ipa lori ọna `DELETE /api/v1/batches/delete-completed` onišẹ ti nfa, eyiti o duro lainidi (ko si àlẹmọ ọjọ-ori) nipasẹ apẹrẹ.                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `BATCH_AND_FILE_AUTO_CLEANUP_ENABLED`      | `false`                       | `src/lib/db/cleanup.ts`                               | Nigba ti `true`, jẹ ki ifasilẹ aifọwọyi laifọwọyi pa awọn iṣẹ Batch API ebute (ati awọn aaye ayẹwo wọn) kọja `OMNIROUTE_BATCH_RETENTION_DAYS`, ki o si ko akoonu BLOB ti awọn faili ti o ti gbejade kọja `expires_at` tiwọn. Paa nipa aiyipada: gbogbo fifi sori ẹrọ ti o wa tẹlẹ n tọju data yii ni deede bi iṣaaju titi oniṣẹ ẹrọ yoo fi wọle. Bakannaa asia ẹya dashboard-editable — wo `docs/reference/FEATURE_FLAGS.md` → Runtime.                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_DB_HEALTHCHECK`            | `0`                           | `src/lib/db/core.ts`, `src/lib/db/healthCheck.ts`     | Ṣeto si `1` lati foju sọwedowo ilera DB patapata ni ibẹrẹ. Wulo fun awọn iṣẹ ṣiṣe igba diẹ ati awọn idanwo isọpọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_FORCE_DB_HEALTHCHECK`           | `0`                           | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Ṣeto si `1` lati fi ipa mu loop iṣayẹwo ilera DB lori, paapaa nigba ti yoo jẹ deede fo (fun apẹẹrẹ, awọn iṣẹ ṣiṣe igba diẹ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_POSTINSTALL`               | `0`                           | `scripts/postinstall.mjs`                             | Ṣeto si `1` lati fo igbona akoko-akoko abinibi lakoko `npm install`. Wulo ni awọn fifi sori ẹrọ CI/headless nibiti a ti kọ sqlite tẹlẹ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_MIGRATIONS_DIR`                 | _(Ṣawari-laifọwọyi)_          | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner.ts`                       | Daju liana ti olusare iṣiwa naa ṣayẹwo. Wulo nigbati o ba nfi awọn iṣipopo pọ si ni awọn itumọ aṣa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_EXTRA_MIGRATIONS_DIRS`          | _(a ko ṣeto)_                 | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner/extraDirs.ts`             | Awọn ilana iṣilọ ni afikun bi awọn titẹ sii `namespace=dir` ti o yapa nipasẹ ọna ipa ọna pẹpẹ (fun apẹẹrẹ `ee=/opt/app/enterprise/db/migrations`). Awọn faili ti o rii nibẹ ti wa ni igbasilẹ bi `<namespace>-<number>`, nitorinaa pinpin gbigbe awọn ijira tirẹ ko kọlu pẹlu awọn iho nomba oke. Titẹwọle aiṣedeede, aaye orukọ ti ko tọ tabi itọsọna ti o padanu n jabọ ni ibẹrẹ dipo fifikọ silẹ ni ipalọlọ naa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_MAX_PENDING_MIGRATIONS`         | `50`                          | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner.ts`                       | Ibi-iduro-ni isunmọtosi-iṣira ibi aabo (#3416). Bibẹrẹ aborts ti diẹ sii ju eyi ọpọlọpọ awọn ijira wa ni isunmọtosi lori DB ti o wa tẹlẹ (awọn olusona lodi si tabili ipasẹ parẹ). Gbe soke lati mu pada agbalagba afẹyinti; ṣeto si `0` lati mu ayẹwo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_INSTALL_UPGRADE_WORKDIR`        | _(`<repo>/.install-upgrade`)_ | `scripts/check/check-install-upgrade.mjs`             | Ilana iṣẹ fun ẹnu-ọna idasilẹ `check:install-upgrade`. O nilo aijọju 12 GB (meji ~ 3 GB fi sori ẹrọ igi pẹlu tarball), nitorina ko gbọdọ ṣiṣẹ lori tmpfs — kekere kan lori olusare ti ara ẹni `/tmp` jẹ tmpfs 12 GB Ramu ti o ṣe atilẹyin ati ẹnu-bode naa ti rẹ, gige package naa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_SPEND_FLUSH_INTERVAL_MS`        | _(aiyipada ni koodu)_         | `src/lib/spend/batchWriter.ts`                        | Flush aarin (ms) fun awọn ipele spend/cost onkqwe. Isalẹ iye din kikọ coalescing; ti o ga iye din DB ariyanjiyan.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_SPEND_MAX_BUFFER_SIZE`          | _(aiyipada ni koodu)_         | `src/lib/spend/batchWriter.ts`                        | Awọn titẹ sii inawo ti o pọju ṣaaju ki o to fi agbara mu fifọ. Dide lori awọn imuṣiṣẹ giga-QPS; kekere nigbati iranti didi ṣe pataki diẹ sii.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_FETCH_DEBUG`              | _(a ko ṣeto)_                 | `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`                        | Ṣeto si `"true"` lati gbe awọn iwe yokokoro `[ProxyFetch]` jade lori ọna yiyi Vercel. Paa nipa aiyipada lati yago fun jijo ipa ọna.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_CORE_BINARY_PATH`         | _(a ko ṣeto)_                 | `src/lib/proxySubscription/coreConfig/sync.ts`        | Ọna pipe ti alakomeji mojuto aṣoju aṣoju agbegbe (faili kan ti a npè ni `sing-box` / `mihomo`) ti o ṣayẹwo iṣeto ipilẹ ti ipilẹṣẹ ṣaaju ki o rọpo faili ti o gba. Unset kọ `<path>.generated` lẹgbẹẹ faili ti o gba nikan. Ayika agbalejo nikan: ko tii fipamọ sinu aaye data tabi gba nipasẹ iṣakoso API, nitori alakomeji ti wa ni ṣiṣe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_CORE_RELOAD_COMMAND`      | _(a ko ṣeto)_                 | `src/lib/proxySubscription/coreConfig/reload.ts`      | Bawo ni mojuto aṣoju agbegbe ṣe tun gbejade lẹhin rirọpo atunto atunto kan: ariyanjiyan JSON kan nṣiṣẹ laisi ikarahun kan, tabi `external` gangan nigbati oluwoye ita ba lo faili naa. Maṣe fipamọ sinu ibi ipamọ data tabi farapa nipasẹ API.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `PROXY_LOG_INCLUDE_IPS`                    | `false`                       | `src/lib/proxyLogger.ts`                              | Ṣeto si `"true"` tabi `"1"` lati ṣafikun client/egress IPs ati ami-iṣaaju akọọlẹ ninu laini ilana ilana-ọrọ ọrọ-ọrọ `[ProxyEgress]`. Ti tọju OFF nipasẹ aiyipada ki akọọlẹ ilana naa ko jo IPs tabi ami-iṣaaju akọọlẹ naa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_DEBUG`                          | _(a ko ṣeto)_                 | `bin/cli/commands/quota.mjs`                          | Ṣeto si `1` lati tẹ awọn iwadii akoko ibeere fun-kọọkan (`[omniroute] GET <path> completed in Nms`) lati awọn pipaṣẹ ipin CLI si stderr.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEALTHCHECK_PATH`               | _(laifọwọyi)_                 | `scripts/dev/healthcheck.mjs`                         | Ọna ti o fojuhan ti a ṣe ayẹwo nipasẹ ayẹwo ilera eiyan. Ti ko ṣeto, iwadii naa n gba lati `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`; eto ti o jáde pada sinu jin monitoring endpoint.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_DEBUG_COMPLETION`               | _(a ko ṣeto)_                 | `bin/cli/commands/completion.mjs`                     | Ṣeto si eyikeyi iye ti ko ṣofo lati gbe awọn iwadii `[omniroute completion]` jade lati awọn ọna kaṣe ikarahun-ipari CLI (read/refresh/write). Paa nipasẹ aiyipada — awọn cache yẹn kuna ni ipalọlọ nitorina kaṣe missing/corrupt ko ṣe adehun ipari-taabu rara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `BATCH_RETRY_DURATION_MS`                  | `86400000` (wakati 24)        | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Ferese atunwi ti o pọju fun awọn ohun ipele kọọkan (ms). Awọn nkan ti o kọja iye akoko yii jẹ samisi kuna.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `BATCH_BACKOFF_BASE_MS`                    | `5000`                        | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Idaduro ipilẹ (ms) fun ifẹhinti ipari lori awọn igbiyanju ohun elo ipele.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `BATCH_BACKOFF_MAX_MS`                     | `3600000` (1h)                | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Fila (ms) fun ifẹhinti pẹlẹbẹ laarin awọn igbiyanju ohun elo ipele.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `BATCH_MAX_CONCURRENT`                     | `1`                           | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Nọmba ti o pọju ti awọn ipele ti ni ilọsiwaju ni igbakanna. Gbe soke lati mu losi; pa kekere lati yago fun oṣuwọn-iye iji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Ṣaaju ki o to yi iyipada data ti o tẹpẹlẹ ti o wa tẹlẹ, olusare iṣiwa ṣe atẹjade pipe, aworan ti a koju akoonu labẹ `DATA_DIR/db_backups/`. Itẹjade nilo eto faili kan ti o ṣe atilẹyin eto-faili kanna, ko si awọn ọna asopọ lile atunkọ pẹlu amuṣiṣẹpọ faili ti o tọ. awọn ọmọ ogun POSIX tun nilo amuṣiṣẹpọ liana; lori Windows, Node le kọ awọn imudani liana, nitorina OmniRoute ṣan faili ti a tẹjade ati ṣe itọju amuṣiṣẹpọ-titẹsi liana bi igbiyanju ti o dara julọ. Ti `DATA_DIR` ti a gbe soke ko ba le pese awọn iṣeduro yẹn, ibẹrẹ kuna ni pipade ṣaaju lilo ijira kan. Gbe `DATA_DIR` lọ si iwọn didun pẹlu awọn alakoko yẹn; maṣe lo `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP` lati fori aabo ijira.

### Awọn oju iṣẹlẹ

| Oju iṣẹlẹ               | Iṣeto ni                                                                                |
| ----------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Idagbasoke agbegbe**  | Fi gbogbo awọn aiyipada silẹ. DB ngbe ni `~/.omniroute/omniroute.db`.                   |
| **Docker**              | `DATA_DIR=/data` + gbe iwọn didun soke ni `/data`.                                      |
| **Ti paroko ni isinmi** | Ṣeto `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY` + tọju awọn afẹyinti ti bọtini! Pipadanu o = padanu data. |
| **CI/Testing**          | `DATA_DIR=/tmp/omniroute-test` — ephemeral, ko si fifi ẹnọ kọ nkan ti o nilo.           |

---

## 3. Nẹtiwọọki & Awọn ibudo

| Ayípadà                                     | Aiyipada                                | Faili orisun                                                             | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PORT`                                      | `20128`                                 | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Ibudo akọkọ fun Dashboard UI mejeeji ati awọn aaye ipari API (ipo ibudo ẹyọkan).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`                       | _(gbongbo = ofo)_                       | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/docker/ensure-docker-base-path.mjs`          | URL subpath fun sìn OmniRoute lẹhin aṣoju yiyipada (ṣeto Next.js `basePath`; awọn àtúnjúwe auth jẹ basePath-aware). Fun apẹẹrẹ `/omniroute`. Ni Docker iye ti wa ni ndin nigba `docker build` (`ARG OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`); Awọn aworan gbongbo ti a ti kọ tẹlẹ le lo iye akoko asiko ti o yatọ ni ẹẹkan ni eiyan bẹrẹ ṣaaju bata bata Next.js. Ṣeto `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` si orisun ita gbangba pẹlu ipa ọna abẹlẹ kanna.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`           | _(gbongbo = ofo)_                       | `src/shared/hooks/useDisplayBaseUrl.ts`                                  | Digi ti o han ni aṣawakiri ti `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`, inlined ni akoko kikọ nitoribẹẹ ifihan aaye ipari dasibodu fihan `https://host/omniroute/v1` dipo `https://host/v1`. Ṣubu pada si `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH` nigbati a ko ṣeto. Tunṣe lẹhin iyipada ( `basePath` to nbọ jẹ akoko kikọ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `DASHBOARD_ALLOW_EMBED`                     | _(a ko ṣeto = kii ṣe framable)_         | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/dashboardEmbed.mjs`                    | Jade iframe ifibọ ti awọn oju-iwe HTML. Ti ko ṣeto, gbogbo ipa ọna ọkọ `frame-ancestors 'none'` + `X-Frame-Options: DENY`. Ṣeto si `vscode` lati sin awọn oju-iwe naa (dasibodu, buwolu wọle, awọn iwe aṣẹ, ibalẹ) pẹlu `frame-ancestors 'self' vscode-webview:` ko si si `X-Frame-Options`, nitorinaa koodu VS Rrọrun aṣawakiri le fun wọn (ipo OmniCopilot's `dashboardOpen: "editor"` ). Dada API (`/api`, `/v1`, `/v1beta`, `/a2a`, `/healthz`, aliases ipele root ) tọju awọn akọle ti o muna ni ọna boya. `vscode` nikan ni a mọ — `1`/`true` ko mu ṣiṣẹ. Akoko Kọ: tun ṣe lẹhin iyipada (`docker build --build-arg DASHBOARD_ALLOW_EMBED=vscode` fun awọn aworan; siseto lori fifi sori ẹrọ ti a ti kọ tẹlẹ ko ni ipa).                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `API_PORT`                                  | _(a ko ṣeto)_                           | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Nigbati o ba ṣeto, ṣe iranṣẹ `/v1/*` aṣoju API (pẹlu Gemini-abinibi `/v1beta/*`) lori ibudo lọtọ yii.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `API_HOST`                                  | `0.0.0.0`                               | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Di adirẹsi fun API ibudo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `DASHBOARD_PORT`                            | _(a ko ṣeto)_                           | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Nigbati o ba ṣeto, ṣe iranṣẹ Dashboard UI lori ibudo lọtọ yii.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNI_MAX_CONCURRENT_CONNECTIONS`           | `0` _(alaabo)_                          | `src/sse/utils/backpressure.ts`                                          | Awọn asopọ ibaraẹnisọrọ inu-ofurufu nigbakanna awọn fila; awọn ibeere lori fila gba `503` pẹlu `Retry-After`. Odidi odidi jẹ ki oluso; unset/`0` mu u.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_INSTANCE_ID`                     | _(a ko ṣeto)_                           | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`                                   | Idurosinsin, ID alailẹgbẹ fun ẹnu-ọna yii nigbati o ba n di awọn apẹẹrẹ OmniRoute. Ṣiṣe awọn sọwedowo-lupu ẹlẹgbẹ ti nwọle. Awọn lẹta ti a gba laaye: awọn lẹta, awọn nọmba, `.`, `_`, `:`, ati `-`; o pọju 64 ohun kikọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_URLS`                       | _(a ko ṣeto)_                           | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`, `open-sse/executors/base.ts`     | OmniRoute ipilẹ URLs ti o ya sọtọ ti o le gba `X-OmniRoute-Peer-Trace`. Nikan ni a gba laaye ni gbangba ni oke URLs gba metadata ẹlẹgbẹ; gbogbo awọn olupese miiran ko fọwọkan.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_MAX_HOPS`                   | `4`                                     | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`                                   | Nọmba ti o pọ julọ ti awọn apẹẹrẹ OmniRoute ti ṣabẹwo si iṣaaju ti gba lori ibeere ti a dè (`1`-`32`). Awọn iṣẹlẹ ti a tun sọ tabi isuna ti o rẹ pada HTTP `508 Loop Detected`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `PROD_DASHBOARD_PORT`                       | `20130`                                 | `docker-compose.prod.yml`                                                | Ibudo atẹjade ẹgbẹ-ogun fun Dasibodu ni ipo iṣelọpọ Docker.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `PROD_API_PORT`                             | `20131`                                 | `docker-compose.prod.yml`                                                | Ibudo atẹjade ẹgbẹ-ogun fun API ni ipo iṣelọpọ Docker.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_PORT`                            | _(a ko ṣeto)_                           | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | O gba iṣaaju lori `PORT` nigbati o nṣiṣẹ inu Electron tabi awọn ohun elo miiran.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `LIVE_WS_PORT`                              | `20129`                                 | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts`                                            | Ibudo fun olupin ibojuwo ifiwe WebSocket gidi-akoko.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `LIVE_WS_HOST`                              | `127.0.0.1`                             | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts`                                            | Di adirẹsi fun ifiwe WebSocket olupin. Ṣeto si `0.0.0.0` lati fi han lori LAN (tun tunto `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`). Awọn isopọ jẹri pẹlu kuki igba dasibodu tabi bọtini API Bearer ti o di aaye iṣakoso kan mu (`manage`/`admin`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`                   | _(a ko ṣeto)_                           | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts`                                            | Awọn ipilẹṣẹ afikun-iyapa-koma gba laaye lati ṣii WebSocket laaye. Awọn ipilẹṣẹ dasibodu Loopback ti gba laaye tẹlẹ nipasẹ aiyipada.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_HOSTS`                     | _(a ko ṣeto)_                           | `src/server/ws/liveServerAllowList.ts`                                   | Awọn orukọ agbalejo afikun-iyapa-koma gba laaye fun awọn ipilẹṣẹ WebSocket laaye. Ko dabi `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS` (orisun URLs ni kikun), ibaamu nikan ni apa agbalejo — wulo fun awọn iṣeto LAN/Tailscale.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_LIVE_WS_PUBLIC_URL`            | _(a ko ṣeto)_                           | `src/hooks/useLiveDashboard.ts`                                          | URL ti gbogbo eniyan fun dasibodu ifiwe WebSocket (agbegbe aṣawakiri). Ṣeto nigba iwaju olupin WS pẹlu aṣoju yiyipada tabi Eefin Cloudflare (fun apẹẹrẹ `wss://ws.my-ai.com/live-ws`); ẹrọ aṣawakiri naa sopọ nibẹ dipo `ws://hostname:20132`. Apa ipa ọna naa tun lo bi ọna igbesoke WebSocket (aiyipada: `/live-ws`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_ENABLE_LIVE_WS`                  | `true`                                  | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts` ati `scripts/start-ws-server.mjs`          | Ṣeto si `0` tabi `false` lati mu olupin WebSocket gidi-gidi jẹ (ṣiṣẹ nipasẹ aiyipada, loopback-bound). CI/harness toggle ti o mu iwe afọwọkọ oluranlọwọ WebSocket laaye laaye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `RELAY_IP_PER_MINUTE`                       | `30`                                    | `src/app/api/v1/relay/chat/completions/route.ts`                         | Per- (tokini, IP) opin oṣuwọn yiyi, requests/minute. Ninu-iranti, fun apẹẹrẹ. `0` tabi odi pa ẹnu-ọna iwọn-IP (fun-àmi DB opin si tun kan).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `NODE_ENV`                                  | `production`                            | Next.js mojuto                                                           | Ṣakoso ọrọ sisọ gedu, caching, ifihan alaye aṣiṣe, ati awọn iṣapeye Next.js.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_USE_TURBOPACK`                   | _(ti ko ṣeto) _ (aṣayan kọ adaṣe adaṣe) | `scripts/build/build-next-isolated.mjs`                                  | Fun `npm run build`, ṣiṣi silẹ tabi ofo yan apo wẹẹbu nigbati opin iranti to munadoko ( RAM ti ara tabi ipin cgroup/process isalẹ) wa ni pupọ julọ** 16 GiB** tabi ko le ṣe ipinnu lailewu; loke 16 GiB, Turbopack ti yan. Apopọ wẹẹbu ologun `0` ti o han gbangba; `1` ti o fojuhan ati awọn iye ti kii ṣe ofo miiran ipa Turbopack. Iye ayika ti o ṣofo ti a firanṣẹ siwaju nipasẹ Ṣajọ ṣe** kii ṣe** agbara Turbopack. Ibalẹ yii wa lati 15.6 GiB Kọ-iranti ipadasẹhin (# 18184); ogun kọ pẹlu 24/32 GiB won ko ala. Apopọ wẹẹbu iṣelọpọ laifọwọyi foju kaṣe itẹramọṣẹ rẹ nipasẹ aiyipada (wo `OMNIROUTE_WEBPACK_BUILD_CACHE` ni isalẹ; #18141) ati kọ pẹlu RSS tente ti o kere pupọ ju ọna Turbopack lọwọlọwọ lọ. Idagbasoke da duro awọn oniwe-tẹlẹ lapapo ihuwasi.**Docker ti kọ aiyipada tẹlẹ si `0` (packweb)** ; lo `--build-arg OMNIROUTE_USE_TURBOPACK=1` lati jade. Wo `docs/guides/DOCKER_GUIDE.md`. |
| `OMNIROUTE_WEBPACK_BUILD_CACHE`             | _(a ko ṣeto)_                           | `next.config.mjs`                                                        | Apopọ wẹẹbu n ṣe agbero (`OMNIROUTE_USE_TURBOPACK=0`) foo apo-iwe wẹẹbu itẹramọṣẹ kaṣe eto faili ayafi ti eyi jẹ `1`. Kikọ kaṣe yẹn ṣe afikun nipa 2.5 GB ti iranti itumọ ti tente oke ni 6144 MB okiti okiti, ati awọn ile mimọ ko ka pada. Ṣeto si `1` lati tọju `.build/next/cache/webpack` fun ṣiṣe atunṣe yiyara lori ẹrọ kan pẹlu RAM lati sa; Docker kọ gba bi `--build-arg OMNIROUTE_WEBPACK_BUILD_CACHE=1` (`Dockerfile` ati `Dockerfile.bun` awọn ipele ọmọle).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_DB_HEALTHCHECK`             | _(a ko ṣeto)_                           | `src/lib/db/core.ts` / `src/lib/db/healthCheck.ts`                       | Ṣeto si `1` lati fo SQLite iṣotitọ ilera ayẹwo ni ibẹrẹ. Wulo fun yiyara bata lori awọn apoti isura infomesonu nla.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `NOTIFY_SOCKET`                             | _(a ko ṣeto)_                           | systemd (ilana sd_notify)                                                | Ṣeto nipasẹ eto nigbati ilana naa ba ṣiṣẹ labẹ ẹyọ iṣẹ kan pẹlu iṣọpọ sd_notify; OmniRoute ka (wo `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_SD_NOTIFY`) lati fi awọn iwifunni READY/WATCHDOG ranṣẹ. Maṣe ṣeto nipasẹ olumulo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_SD_NOTIFY`               | _(a ko ṣeto)_                           | `scripts/dev/systemd-notify.mjs`                                         | Ṣeto si `1` lati mu systemd sd_notify (Iru = notify / WatchdogSec=) paapaa nigba ti o nṣiṣẹ labẹ ẹyọ ti eto. Awọn notifier ni a ko si-op ita systemd laiwo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
