| `PIN_DROP_BACKOFF_LEVEL`                                      | `2`       | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`                         | Ìpele backoff tí a máa ka olupese pin cache àyíká sí ẹni tí kò ní ìlera pẹ́ títí, tí a sì máa yọ pin náà kúrò fún failover.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `PIN_DROP_GRACE_MS`                                           | `20000`   | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`                         | Àkókò ìdènà yíyí-yípadà (ms) tí ń fara gba àwọn àkókò ìtútù kéékèèké fún ìgbà díẹ̀ kí a tó yọ pin cache àyíká kúrò.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

### Àwọn Àyẹ̀wò

| Àyẹ̀wò                                     | Àtòjọpọ̀                                                     |
| ----------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Ìṣẹ̀dá kóòdù tó ń gba àkókò pípẹ́**       | `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS=900000` (ìṣẹ́jú 15)                      |
| **Ìgbésí-ayé odò lapapọ̀ tí a fi ààlà sí** | `STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS=1260000` (ìṣẹ́jú 21)               |
| **Ìkùnà kíákíá fún API iṣelọpọ**          | `API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS=10000`                         |
| **Àwọn modeli ìrònú tó gbooro**           | `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS=300000` (ìṣẹ́jú 5 láàárín àwọn ìpín) |

## 16. Wọle

Eto gedu kọwe si mejeeji stdout ati awọn faili log ti yiyi. Gbogbo iṣeto ni a ka nipasẹ `src/lib/logEnv.ts`.

| Ayípadà                                   | Aiyipada                   | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ----------------------------------------- | -------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `APP_LOG_LEVEL`                           | `info`                     | Ipele akọọlẹ ti o kere julọ: `debug`, `info`, `warn`, `error`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `APP_LOG_FORMAT`                          | `text`                     | Ọna kika jade: `text` (eniyan-ṣe kika) tabi `json` (ti a ṣeto).                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `APP_LOG_TO_FILE`                         | `true`                     | Kọ awọn akọọlẹ lati faili lẹgbẹẹ stdout.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `APP_LOG_FILE_PATH`                       | `logs/application/app.log` | Ọna faili Wọle (i ibatan si root akanṣe tabi `DATA_DIR`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `APP_LOG_MAX_FILE_SIZE`                   | `50M`                      | Iwọn faili ti o pọju ṣaaju iyipo. Gba: `50M`, `1G`, `512K`, tabi awọn baiti pẹtẹlẹ.                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `APP_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`                  | `7`                        | Awọn ọjọ lati tọju awọn faili log log ti yiyi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `APP_LOG_MAX_FILES`                       | `20`                       | Awọn afẹyinti faili log yiyipo ti o pọju.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CALL_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`                 | `7`                        | Awọn ọjọ lati tọju awọn titẹ sii request/call sinu ibi ipamọ data.                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `CALL_LOG_MAX_ENTRIES`                    | `10000`                    | Awọn titẹ sii ipe wọle ti o pọju ninu ifipamọ inu-iranti.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CALL_LOGS_TABLE_MAX_ROWS`                | `100000`                   | Awọn ori ila ti o pọju ninu tabili `call_logs` SQLite ṣaaju ki o to pruning.                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `ENABLE_REQUEST_LOGS`                     | _(a ko ṣeto)_              | Fi ipa mu ibeere alaye wọle si tan tabi paa, ti o bori eto dasibodu naa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `MAX_PENDING_REQUEST_AGE_MS`              | `3600000` (wakati 1)       | Ọjọ ori ti o pọ julọ fun awọn titẹ sii iforukọsilẹ ibeere alainibaba ti nṣiṣe lọwọ ṣaaju ṣiṣe mimọ inu-iranti.                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `MAX_PENDING_DETAILS`                     | `5000`                     | Awọn alaye isunmọtosi ti o pọju (odidi to dara); fila kika: gba; awọn fila byte/map : fi sii.                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CALL_LOG_PIPELINE_CAPTURE_STREAM_CHUNKS` | `false`                    | Tọju awọn ege ṣiṣan ni awọn ohun-ọṣọ opo gigun ti epo nigbati `call_log_pipeline_enabled=true`. Jade-inu (`true`) — pipa nipasẹ aiyipada lati fi disk pamọ.                                                                                                                                  |
| `CALL_LOG_PIPELINE_MAX_SIZE_KB`           | `512`                      | Iwọn pipe pipe pipe log artifact iwọn ni KB nigbati `call_log_pipeline_enabled=true`.                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `PROXY_LOGS_TABLE_MAX_ROWS`               | `100000`                   | Awọn ori ila ti o pọju ninu tabili `proxy_logs` SQLite ṣaaju ki o to pruning.                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `PROXY_LOG_INCLUDE_IPS`                   | `false`                    | Fi client/egress IPs ati awọn ami-iṣaaju akọọlẹ sinu awọn akọọlẹ console `[ProxyEgress]`. Awọn igbasilẹ dashboard/database aṣoju-log n ṣe idaduro awọn alaye ni kikun.                                                                                                                       |
| `PROXY_LOG_FIRST_CHUNK_TIMING`            | `false`                    | Ṣeto si `"true"` tabi `"1"` lati ṣe igbasilẹ `first_chunk_ms` (firanṣẹ ibere si baiti ara iwulo akọkọ) lori awọn ori ila aṣoju-log. Murasilẹ kọọkan soke ara ni a ọlẹ passthrough san ati abulẹ awọn kana ni kete ti awọn baiti de; pipa nipa aiyipada ki awọn idahun kọja nipasẹ untouched. |
| `APP_LOG_ROTATION_CHECK_INTERVAL_MS`      | `60000` (1 min)            | Igba melo ni `src/lib/logRotation.ts` tun ṣayẹwo iwọn faili log ti nṣiṣe lọwọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CHAT_LOG_TEXT_LIMIT`                     | `65536`                    | Ipari okun ti o pọju ni idaduro ni awọn ohun elo akọọlẹ iwiregbe (aiyipada 64 KB).                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `CHAT_LOG_ARRAY_TAIL_ITEMS`               | `128`                      | Nọmba awọn ohun orun ti o wa ni idaduro lati iru nigbati o ba npa awọn fifuye akọọlẹ iwiregbe ge.                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_DEPTH`                      | `6`                        | Ijinle itẹ-ẹiyẹ ti o pọ julọ ṣaaju ki awọn ẹru isanwo log iwiregbe ti ge ge.                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_OBJECT_KEYS`                | `80`                       | Awọn bọtini ohun ti o pọju ti o wa ni idaduro ni awọn fifuye akọọlẹ iwiregbe (0 = ailopin).                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_BODY_KB`                    | `1024`                     | Gbogbo iwọn ara request/response (KB) ṣaaju ki o to rọpo rẹ nipasẹ akopọ igboro dipo ẹda oniye kikun. Gbe eyi soke ti awọn ibaraẹnisọrọ aṣoju gigun ba fihan ibi ipamọ dipo awọn ifiranṣẹ gidi ninu dasibodu naa.                                                                            |
| `CHAT_DEBUG_FILE`                         | `false`                    | Nigbati o ba jẹ otitọ, `serializeArtifactForStorage` foju iwọn-ti o da lori iwọn. Ṣatunkọ nikan.                                                                                                                                                                                             |

## 17. Memory o dara ju

| Ayípadà                                      | Aiyipada                                                                                  | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| -------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB`                        | _auto_ (irin igboro);**`1024` ninu aworan Docker**                                        | **Ti ṣe iṣeduro** Docker/standalone V8 òkiti ààlà (MB). Nigbati a ko ṣeto, ti ṣe iwọn ni agbara (~ 35% ti eto RAM, dimọ si `[512, 4096]`); `512` jẹ ilẹ nikan nigbati iranti lapapọ ko le ka. Lori `run-standalone.mjs` (Docker CMD), an** fojuhan** iye ti wa ni appended bi `--max-old-space-size` ati** bori** lori asia òkiti NODE_OPTIONS rogbodiyan (V8 kẹhin-flag). `omniroute serve` tun fẹran òkiti NODE_OPTIONS ti o wa tẹlẹ (#5238). Maṣe ṣeto awọn mejeeji si awọn nọmba oriṣiriṣi — ilana naa ṣe igbasilẹ ikilọ kan lorukọ awọn iye mejeeji ati olubori.** Aworan Docker osise nigbagbogbo n ṣeto `1024`, nitorinaa isọdọtun ko ṣiṣẹ nibẹ.** Ifaminsi-oluranlowo `/v1/responses` nilo `8192`–`12288` plus cgroup headroom — wo [Docker Itọsọna — asiko isise RAM](../guides/DOCKER_GUIDE.md#runtime-ram-for-coding-agents). |
| `OMNIROUTE_RSS_PRESSURE_MB`                  | _auto_ (`max(3072, 2 × OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB)`; pipa nigbati `OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB` ko ṣeto) | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressure.ts`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | Ilana pipe-fiusi RSS (MB) fun iranti native/external ti V8 `heapUsed` ko le ri; loke rẹ awọn ibeere titun gba 503 `resource_pressure`. RSS ṣọwọn silẹ lẹhin iwasoke, nitorinaa so pọ pẹlu `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART`. |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_SIZE`                      | `50`                                                                                      | Ti o pọju awọn titẹ sii tọ eto cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_BYTES`                     | `2097152` (2 MB)                                                                          | Iwọn kaṣe kiakia lapapọ lapapọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_TTL_MS`                        | `300000` (5 min)                                                                          | Titẹ sii kaṣe kiakia TTL.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_SIZE`                    | `100`                                                                                     | Iwọn otutu ti o pọju = 0 awọn idahun.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_BYTES`                   | `4194304` (4 MB)                                                                          | Iwọn kaṣe atunmọ lapapọ lapapọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_TTL_MS`                      | `1800000` (30 min)                                                                        | Titẹ sii kaṣe atunmọ TTL.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_SEMANTIC_CACHE_PRUNE_INTERVAL_MS` | `1800000` (30 min)                                                                        | SQLite prune aarin. `0` ṣe piparẹ ibẹrẹ ati awọn ṣiṣe igbakọọkan; awọn iye miiran ni isalẹ `60000` ṣubu pada si aiyipada. Awọn iye ti o wa loke `2147483647` ms dimole si idaduro aago Node ti o pọju, yago fun iṣan omi si ibo ibo 1ms. Akọkọ ṣiṣe lẹhin 1s; Aago ni o wa unref'd ati awọn gbalaye kò ni lqkan.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_SEMANTIC_CACHE_PRUNE_BATCH_SIZE`  | `2000`                                                                                    | Awọn ori ila fun SQLite prune DELETE, dimọ si `[100, 50000]`; Egbin si iṣẹlẹ iṣẹlẹ laarin awọn ipele.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_SEMANTIC_CACHE_PRUNE_MAX_BATCHES` | `50`                                                                                      | Eto isuna ipele DELETE o pin fun ṣiṣe kọja ipari ipari ati iyan gba ila-fila, dimọ si `[1, 10000]`. Awọn ifẹhinti tẹsiwaju lori awọn ṣiṣe nigbamii.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_ROWS`          | `0`                                                                                       | Iyan SQLite fila kana (`0` = kò si). Lẹhin piparẹ `expires_at <=` naa bẹrẹ-bẹrẹ ISO timestamp, ko jade `created_at` atijọ , lẹhinna `rowid`, pẹlu awọn ori ila laaye, koko-ọrọ si isuna ipele ipin. Awọn iye ti wa ni gedu si awọn odidi (titi de opin nomba ailewu). Ofo, ti kii-nọmba, ti kii-ipin tabi awọn iye odi fun gbogbo awọn oniyipada piruni mẹrin ṣubu pada si awọn aṣiṣe wọn; ida yika si isalẹ. Pruning ko yi TTL, iranti LRU tabi ihuwasi kaṣe vector.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_CORPUS_CACHE_SIZE`                | `5`                                                                                       | Awọn gbongbo agbegbe-corpus ti o tọju itọka iranti laaye ni ẹẹkan (`src/lib/localCorpus/configured.ts`). LRU: ni opin iwọn atọka root ti o kere julọ-laipe-lo ti yọ kuro ati tun ṣe lori ibeere atẹle rẹ. Dimọ si o kere ju `1`; iye ti kii ṣe nomba ṣubu pada si aiyipada.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `STREAM_HISTORY_MAX`                         | `50`                                                                                      | Awọn iṣẹlẹ ṣiṣan aipẹ ti o pọju ni ifipamọ wiwo ifiwe Dashboard.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CONTEXT_LENGTH_DEFAULT`                     | _(a ko ṣeto)_                                                                             | Ipari ọrọ-ọrọ agbaye-ipari (awọn ami ami) fun gbogbo olupese ti ko ni oniyipada `CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>`. Nigbati o ba ṣeto, o** rọpo** katalogi, dasibodu dasibodu ati awọn iye iforukọsilẹ fun awọn olupese wọnyẹn; nigbati a ko ṣeto, OmniRoute nlo pq ti a ṣe sinu rẹ, eyiti ibi-afẹde ikẹhin rẹ jẹ `128000`. Wo [ipari ọrọ-ọrọ fun Olupese](#per-provider-context-length-context_length_provider).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `USAGE_TOKEN_BUFFER`                         | `100`                                                                                     | Yara ori ami afikun ti o wa ni ipamọ nigba titọpa awọn ipin lilo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

### Gigun ọrọ-ọrọ fun Olupese (`CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>`)

`open-sse/services/contextManager.ts` (`getEnvOverride()` / `resolveTokenLimit()`) tun ka a** fun-olupese** fọọmu ti a kọ orukọ rẹ lati ọdọ olupese ID ni akoko asiko, nitorinaa ko le ṣe atokọ ni ila ni ila ninu tabili loke tabi ni `.env.example`.

**Oruko.** `CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>`, nibiti `<PROVIDER>` ti jẹ olupese ID oke-cased, pẹlu gbogbo ohun kikọ ita `A-Z` / `0-9` rọpo nipasẹ `_`. Olupese ID jẹ ọkan ti ilana lẹhin ipinnu inagijẹ (apakan `provider` ti okun `provider/model` ni kete ti inagijẹ kukuru ti ni ipinnu). Aṣa OpenAI- tabi awọn apa ibaramu Anthropic lo node ti ipilẹṣẹ ID wọn, nitorinaa UUID jẹ apakan ti orukọ oniyipada. Awọn iye ti wa ni a rere odidi nọmba ti àmi; ohunkohun miiran (ofo, `0`, odi, ti kii-nọmba) jẹ aibikita ati ipinnu gbe lọ si igbesẹ ti nbọ.

| Olupese ID                                                  | Orukọ oniyipada                                                            |
| ----------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| oluranlowo olulana                                          | CONTEXT_LENGTH_AGENTROUTER                                                 |
| openrouter                                                  | CONTEXT_LENGTH_OPENROUTER                                                  |
| openai-compatible-chat-0f8e2c1a-5b7d-4e3f-9a6c-2d1b8e4f7a90 | CONTEXT_LENGTH_OPENAI_COMPATIBLE_CHAT_0F8E2C1A_5B7D_4E3F_9A6C_2D1B8E4F7A90 |

**Iwaju.** Fun bata `provider/model`, igbesẹ akọkọ ti o mu iye kan yọrisi:

1. `CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>` fun olupese naa.
2. `CONTEXT_LENGTH_DEFAULT` (agbegbe ayika agbaye).
3. Ferese fun-awoṣe: ipo-ọrọ ti o tẹsiwaju (ṣeto sinu dasibodu, tabi afọwọṣe-pin nipasẹ olulaja wiwa, wo `CONTEXT_WINDOW_RECONCILE_INTERVAL`), bibẹẹkọ katalogi amuṣiṣẹpọ / awọn awoṣe.dev iye.
4. Iforukọsilẹ olupese `defaultContextLength`.
5. Awoṣe-orukọ heuristics (`claude` 200000, `gemini` 1000000, `gpt` / `o1` / `o3` / `o4` / `codex` 400000).
6. Awọn aṣiṣe ti a ṣe sinu fun olupese, lẹhinna `128000`.

Mejeeji ayika awọn igbesẹ ti waye si awọn** gbogbo olupese** : Gbogbo awoṣe ti olupese naa gba opin kanna, ati pe wọn bori lori awọn dasibodu awoṣe-apẹẹrẹ. Nigbati awoṣe kan ti olupese kan nilo window ti o yatọ, lo ifasilẹ fun-apẹẹrẹ ni dasibodu dipo.

**Ààlà.** Ẹwọn yii n ṣakiyesi akoko ibeere-akoko-window ( `context_length_exceeded` / “Input over context window” ijusile ni `open-sse/handlers/chatCore.ts`), awọn isuna funmorawon kiakia ati awọn opin ọrọ-ọrọ konbo. `context_length` ti a polowo nipasẹ `GET /v1/models` tun le wa lati inu iwe akọọlẹ tabi metadata iforukọsilẹ ati pe ko ṣe iṣeduro lati ṣe afihan ipadasẹhin ayika.

**Apeere.** Olupese ti awoṣe rẹ gba ferese ami-ami 1,050,000 nigba ti katalogi ṣe ijabọ 128,000:

```dotenv
CONTEXT_LENGTH_AGENTROUTER=1050000
```

Tun olupin bẹrẹ lẹhin ti o yi pada: iye ti ka lati agbegbe ilana olupin. Lori awọn** tabili app** olupin naa nṣiṣẹ bi ilana ọmọde ati pe ko ri iyipada eto kan lẹhin ti a ti ṣe ifilọlẹ app; fi laini sinu faili `.env` awọn ẹru app (aṣẹ wiwa ni [itọsọna Electron : Ṣiṣayẹwo faili ayika](../guides/ELECTRON_GUIDE.md#environment-file-lookup) ), lẹhinna dawọ kuro ni kikun ki o tun bẹrẹ app naa.

### Funmorawon

| Ayípadà                               | Aiyipada | Apejuwe                                                                                                             |
| ------------------------------------- | -------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_RTK_TRUST_PROJECT_FILTERS` | unset    | Gbẹkẹle ise agbese `.rtk/filters.json` laisi hash `.rtk/trust.json` kan. Lo nikan ni idagbasoke agbegbe ti iṣakoso. |

### Ẹrọ iranti (ètò 21)

### Iye owo lupu iṣẹlẹ ti iranti, awọn ọgbọn, ati isọdọtun ami (#10349)

OmniRoute ni a** nikan Node ilana** . extraction/retrieval iranti, abẹrẹ awọn ọgbọn, ati isọdọtun olupese iṣẹ ṣiṣe lori iyẹn** kanna iṣẹlẹ lupu** bi `GET /healthz` ati dasibodu. Wọn kii ṣe okun oṣiṣẹ.

| Ṣiṣẹ                       | Koodu                              | Aiyipada                                    | Iṣakoso onišẹ                                                                                                                    |
| -------------------------- | ---------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Iranti isediwon / igbapada | `src/lib/memory/`                  | Dasibodu**memoryEnabled** (aiyipada lori)   | Paa** Eto → Memory** . Nibẹ ni ko si lọtọ env pa yipada kọja disabling ẹya-ara ni eto.                                           |
| Ogbon abẹrẹ                | `src/lib/skills/injection.ts`      | Dasibodu**skillsEnabled** (aiyipada lori)   | Paa** Eto → Memory/Skills** (`skillsEnabled`). Awọn bọtini apoti iyanrin ni isalẹ ipaniyan ti a dè nikan lẹhin abẹrẹ ti wa tẹlẹ. |
| Isọdọtun ami               | `src/sse/services/tokenRefresh.ts` | Tan fun awọn olupese OAuth/web ti a ti sopọ | Ge asopọ olupese tabi jẹ ki awọn ami duro wulo; ko si `TOKEN_REFRESH=0` env loni.                                                |

Ti `/healthz` ba lọra lori apoti idakẹjẹ, mu awọn ọgbọn + iranti kuro ni akọkọ, lẹhinna ṣayẹwo fifuye catalog/compression (#10303, #9685). Awọn ẹya wọnyi mu jade ni awọn aaye `await` ṣugbọn ṣi dije fun okun kan.

Ifibọlẹ Layer, ibi-itaja fekito ati awọn bọtini atunto fun eto-iṣẹ iranti itẹramọṣẹ (`src/lib/memory/`).

| Ayípadà                              | Aiyipada                    | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ------------------------------------ | --------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `MEMORY_EMBEDDING_CACHE_TTL_MS`      | `300000` (5 min)            | TTL fun kaṣe ifibọ inu-iranti (fun ibuwọlu source/model/dim ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `MEMORY_EMBEDDING_CACHE_MAX`         | `1000`                      | Awọn titẹ sii LRU ti o pọju ti a fipamọ sinu kaṣe ifibọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `MEMORY_TRANSFORMERS_MODEL`          | `Xenova/all-MiniLM-L6-v2`   | HF repo id fun ijade-inu `@huggingface/transformers` agbegbe opo gigun ti MiniLM (~ 23 MB int8, ~ 400 MB RAM).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `MEMORY_STATIC_MODEL`                | `minishlab/potion-base-8M`  | HF repo id fun iṣipopada tabili wiwa potion/Model2Vec aimi. Ti ṣe igbasilẹ ni ọlẹ sinu kaṣe dir.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `MEMORY_STATIC_CACHE_DIR`            | `<DATA_DIR>/embeddings`     | Liana ti a lo lati kaṣe awọn faili awoṣe potion aimi. Awọn aiyipada labẹ `DATA_DIR` nigbati a ko ṣeto.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `HF_HUB_ENDPOINT`                    | `https://huggingface.co`    | Paarẹ ipilẹ Face Hub ti o famọra URL ti o lo nipasẹ `staticPotion.ts` (fun apẹẹrẹ aaye ipari digi fun awọn iṣeto ti o ni gapped).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `MEMORY_VEC_TOP_K`                   | `20`                        | Oke-K aiyipada ti a lo nipasẹ wiwa `sqlite-vec` brute-force vector inu `src/lib/memory/vectorStore.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `MEMORY_RRF_K`                       | `60`                        | Atunse ipo Fusion ibakan `k` fun arabara FTS5 + fekito igbapada (sqlite-vec ohunelo).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `VECTOR_STORE_DISABLE_VEC`           | `false`                     | Test/diagnostic seaam in `getVectorStore()` (`src/lib/memory/vectorStore.ts`): nigbati `true`, fi agbara mu ibi-itaja vector lati `null` (ṣe afiṣe agbegbe cloud/WASM laisi `sqlite-vec`), imupadabọ iranti abuku si wiwa ọrọ-ọrọ FTS5. Fi aifọkanbalẹ silẹ ni iṣelọpọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `NOTION_API_KEY`                     | _(a ko ṣeto)_               | bọtini API fun ẹyìn Notion (ti a lo nipasẹ `genericBackend.ts` tito tito lẹyin ti a mọ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `NOTION_API_URL`                     | `https://api.notion.com/v1` | Ipilẹ URL fun Notion API (le bori fun awọn ọna yiyan Notion ti ara ẹni ti gbalejo).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OBSIDIAN_API_KEY`                   | _(a ko ṣeto)_               | bọtini API fun Obsidian Vault backend (lo nipasẹ `genericBackend.ts` tito tito lẹyin ti a mọ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OBSIDIAN_API_URL`                   | `http://localhost:27123`    | URL mimọ fun Obsidian Vault API (le bori fun ifinkan latọna jijin).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ENABLED`         | `false`                     | TV6 ti tẹ iranti ibajẹ oluwa yipada.** Jade (pa aiyipada)** — gbigba** npaarẹ** awọn iranti ti bajẹ. Pẹlu piparẹ, `access_count`/`last_accessed_at` jẹ telemetry mimọ ati pe ko si nkankan ti paarẹ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_EPISODIC_DAYS`   | `30`                        | TTL (awọn ọjọ) lẹhin eyi iranti `episodic` ti a ko lo yoo bajẹ. `0` ṣe ajẹsara episodic paapaa. Awọn oriṣi ti o tọ (`factual`/`procedural`/`semantic`) jẹ ajesara nigbagbogbo. Aago ibajẹ tun-ipilẹ lori `last_accessed_at`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ACCESS_IMMUNITY` | `3`                         | Iranti abẹrẹ `>=` yii ni ọpọlọpọ igba di ajesara si ibajẹ laibikita iru. `0` mu ajesara iwọle ṣiṣẹ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_SWEEP_INTERVAL`  | `0` (alaabo)                | Àárín (ìṣẹ́jú àáyá) fún ìparun ìbàjẹ́ ìgbàkọọkan iyan ni `src/lib/memory/typedDecay.ts`. `0`/ unset = ko si gbigba igbakọọkan. Ijade-iwọle lẹmeji: tun nilo `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ENABLED=true`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_STRICT_SYSTEM_PROVIDERS`  | _(a ko ṣeto)_               | Awọn ids olupese ti o ya sọtọ komama (aibikita ọran) ti o gba ifiranṣẹ `system` kan** nikan ni atọka 0** (`src/lib/memory/injection.ts`). Fun iwọnyi, kaṣe-ailewu aarin-array iranti splice jẹ ailewu ni awọn ibaraẹnisọrọ titan-pupọ, nitorinaa iranti jẹ merged/prepended bi ifiranṣẹ eto idari dipo. Awọn aiyipada si `xiaomi-mimo`/`mimo` nikan; faagun fun awọn aaye ipari ibaramu OpenAI ti ara ẹni (fun apẹẹrẹ Qwen3.5 +/ 3.6) eyiti awoṣe iwiregbe nfi idinamọ-ifiranṣẹ eto-asiwaju kanṣoṣo. |

### Kekere-Ramu Docker Apeere

`128` jẹ dasibodu-nikan. Awọn aṣoju ifaminsi lori òkiti `FATAL ERROR` yii lakoko `/v1/responses` gigun. Maṣe lo apẹẹrẹ yii bi ẹnu-ọna Claude/Codex/Grok.

```bash
OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB=128
PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_SIZE=20
PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_BYTES=524288        # 512 KB
SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_SIZE=25
SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_BYTES=1048576     # 1 MB
STREAM_HISTORY_MAX=10
```

| `PROMPTQL_CREDITS_ENDPOINT`  | `https://data.pro.ql.app/v1/graphql`                               | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`, `open-sse/services/usage/promptql.ts` | aaye ipari GraphQL ti a lo lati beere kirẹditi balance/usage.          |
| `PROMPTQL_TOKEN_REFRESH_URL` | `https://auth.pro.ql.app/ddn/project/token`                        | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`                                        | Ipari ipari ti a lo fun isọdọtun-akitiyan tokini.                      |

## Olupese wẹẹbu Adobe Firefly (Unofficial/Experimental)

Itura-itumọ ti ẹrọ aṣawakiri fun olupese wẹẹbu Adobe Firefly (`open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`, `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`, `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyClient.ts`). Iyan — gbogbo awọn aseku wa ni aifwy fun fifi sori tabili deede.

> **Yiyọ kuro ni # 9255.** Akoko asiko asiko ti o somọ CDP atijọ ti Chrome (adobeFireflyChromeRuntime.ts) ti rọpo nipasẹ iṣẹ buwolu aṣawakiri Playwright kan, ati pe awọn koko rẹ ko si mọ. Awọn oniyipada ADOBE_FIREFLY_CHROME_ CDP_PORT / VISIBLE / HEADED / PING / FORCE_RESTART, pẹlu ADOBE_FIREFLY_LOGIN_WAIT_MS ati ADOBE_FIREFLY_FORTER_WAIT_MS, ko ka nibikibi ninu codebase — eto wọn ko ni ipa kankan.

| Ayípadà                              | Aiyipada      | Faili orisun                                    | Apejuwe                                                                                                     |
| ------------------------------------ | ------------- | ----------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_CHROME_HEADLESS`      | `0`           | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts` | Ṣeto si `1` fun Chrome ti ko ni ori otitọ (ti a mọ-fọ fun ipilẹṣẹ; yokokoro nikan).                         |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BROWSER_REFRESH`      | `1`           | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Aṣàwákiri ìṣàfilọ́lẹ̀ tó gbóná opt-in/out. `0` jẹ́ kí gbóná tó máa ṣiṣẹ́ mọ́ (ìmúbọ̀sípò 408 àárín ìpele ṣi kan). |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SESSION_DISK`         | `1`           | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Ṣeto si `0` lati mu idaduro igba Adobe Firefly si disk.                                                     |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_MIN_SUBMIT_GAP_MS`    | _(a ko ṣeto)_ | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Aafo ti o kere ju (ms) ti fi agbara mu laarin awọn ifisilẹ ti o tẹle, ti o bori aiyipada ti a ṣe sinu.      |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BATCH_EXTRA_GAP_MS`   | _(a ko ṣeto)_ | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Aafo afikun (ms) ti a ṣafikun lẹhin ipele aṣeyọri kan, ti o bori aiyipada ti a ṣe sinu.                     |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SUBMIT_BASE_DELAY_MS` | _(a ko ṣeto)_ | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyClient.ts`       | Idaduro ipilẹ (ms) ṣaaju fifisilẹ ibeere iran kan, ti o bori aiyipada ti a ṣe sinu.                         |

## 19. Awoṣe Amuṣiṣẹpọ (Dev)

| Ayípadà                             | Aiyipada            | Faili orisun                       | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ----------------------------------- | ------------------- | ---------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `MODELS_DEV_SYNC_ENABLED`           | _(a ko ṣeto)_       | `src/lib/modelsDevSync.ts`         | Idojuk lile fun modeli.dev amuṣiṣẹpọ ifowoleri. Unset = ola Eto > AI (`modelsDevSyncEnabled`). `0`/`false`/`off`/`no`** bori DB** o si fo mejeeji amuṣiṣẹpọ igbakọọkan ati awọn ọlọjẹ `getModelsDevPricing()` SQL/JSON (imupadabọ nigbati dasibodu ti wa ni wiwọ lori iṣẹlẹ iṣẹlẹ kanna). `1`/`true`/`on`/`yes` awọn ologun amuṣiṣẹpọ lori. Ifowoleri save/clear ṣi pe `backupDbFile("pre-write")`, eyiti ko si-op labẹ isunmi iṣẹju 60 tabi `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP`. |
| `MODELS_DEV_SYNC_INTERVAL`          | `86400` (wakati 24) | `src/lib/modelsDevSync.ts`         | Àkókò ìsiṣẹ́pọ̀ àwòṣe àkópọ̀ ìdàgbàsókè ní ìṣẹ́jú àáyá.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CONTEXT_WINDOW_RECONCILE_INTERVAL` | `86400` (wakati 24) | `src/lib/contextWindowResolver.ts` | Aarin (awọn iṣẹju-aaya) fun atunṣe ara ẹni-atunṣe-window reconciler (5004): awọn olupese pinni-pipe awọn window lati wiwa `/models` bi `auto:discovery` ṣe dojukọ nigbati wọn yapa lati katalogi. Ṣeto si `0` lati mu ṣiṣẹ. Tun lo data ti a muṣiṣẹpọ tẹlẹ (ko si bu tuntun); ko tun kọ `manual` danu.                                                                                                                                                                     |

| `CODEBUDDY_SYSTEM_PROMPT_MAX_LEN`              | ∅ (∞) — _unset = ko si fila ipari_     | `open-sse/executors/codebuddy/shared.ts`                                           | Fila ipari iyan fun awọn eto olupe ti a firanṣẹ si CodeBuddy (CN ati International). Nipa aiyipada nikan ni kiakia ti o gbe ami idanimọ ifaminsi-aṣoju idanimọ jẹ rọpo nipasẹ didoju kan ( àlẹmọ akoonu Tencent kọ iyẹn); gun ta ti wa ni pa. Odidi > 0 tun rọpo eto eto eyikeyi to gun ju ti ọpọlọpọ awọn ohun kikọ lọ; unset, `0` tabi iye ti kii-odidi mu fila. Awọn akọọlẹ rirọpo kọọkan eyiti ofin ti yọ kuro (ami idanimọ tabi ipari). |
| `CLOUDFLARE_API_BASE`                          | `https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4` | `src/app/api/settings/proxy/cloudflare-deploy/route.ts`                            | Pa ipilẹ Cloudflare REST API ti a lo nipasẹ aṣoju-pool Workers imuṣiṣẹ (#4640 / 9router#1360).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

| `NEXT_PUBLIC_CLOUDFLARE_RELAY_ENABLED`         | `true`                                 | `src/app/(dashboard)/dashboard/settings/components/proxy/ProxyPoolTab.tsx`         | Ṣeto si `false` lati tọju aṣayan yiyi Awọn oṣiṣẹ Cloudflare lati taabu Aṣoju Pool.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `CLOUDFLARED_BIN`                              | auto-ri                                | `src/lib/cloudflaredTunnel.ts`                                                     | Ona aṣa si alakomeji `cloudflared`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `CLOUDFLARED_PROTOCOL`                         | `http2`                                | `src/lib/cloudflaredTunnel.ts`                                                     | Ilana gbigbe oju eefin: `http2` (aiyipada), `quic`, tabi `auto`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

Nigbati o ba ṣeto, OmniRoute nṣiṣẹ `tunnel --config <path> run` (ti a npè ni eefin) dipo eefin iyara ephemeral.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |

| `DARIO_HOST`                                   | `127.0.0.1`                            | `open-sse/executors/dario.ts`                                                      | Dario ifibọ-iṣẹ bind/connect ogun (loopback nikan nipasẹ aiyipada).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

| `LOCAL_HOSTNAMES`                              | _(sofo)_                               | `open-sse/config/providerRegistry.ts`                                              | Awọn orukọ agbalejo afikun-iyapa-koma ṣe itọju bi “agbegbe” (awọn orukọ iṣẹ Docker, ati bẹbẹ lọ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

`ENABLE_CC_COMPATIBLE_PROVIDER` jẹ nikan fun awọn relays ẹni-kẹta ti o gba awọn onibara koodu Claude ni iyasọtọ. OmniRoute atunkọ awọn ibeere ki awọn relays wọnyẹn gba wọn. Ti o ba fẹ nikan lo Claude Code CLI, tabi o ko ni idaniloju kini awọn isọdọtun wọnyi jẹ, tọju alaabo yii ki o ṣafikun olupese ibaramu Anthropic deede dipo.

| `PROXY_LATENCY_WINDOW_HOURS`                      | `3`                      | `src/lib/db/proxies.ts`                                                                     | Window akoko (awọn wakati) fun ṣiṣe iṣiro aropin aropin ti awọn aṣoju oludije ni ilana adagun-iṣapeye lairi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_CACHE_TTL_MS`                       | `30000`                  | `src/lib/proxyHealth.ts`                                                                    | Abajade ayẹwo ilera TTL.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `NONE_HONORED_WINDOW_HOURS`                       | `24`                     | `src/lib/db/reasoningEffortCompliance.ts`                                                   | Ferese itọpa ni awọn wakati fun reasoning_effort = ko si oluka ibamu lori GET /v1/models/health.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `NONE_HONORED_CACHE_TTL_MS`                       | `20000`                  | `src/lib/db/reasoningEffortCompliance.ts`                                                   | Kaṣe TTL ninu ms fun aworan ifaramọ ti ko si (15-30s).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `NONE_HONORED_MIN_SAMPLE`                         | `3`                      | `src/lib/db/reasoningEffortCompliance.ts`                                                   | Ko kere ju ti o beere ṣaaju ki awoṣe kan ka ọlá tabi kii ṣe ọlá; ni isalẹ yi awoṣe ka aimọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_UNHEALTHY_CACHE_TTL_MS`             | `2000`                   | `src/lib/proxyHealth.ts`                                                                    | Kaṣe TTL fun awọn iwadii ilera aṣoju ti kuna. Jeki eyi kuru ju `PROXY_HEALTH_CACHE_TTL_MS` nitoribẹẹ awọn akoko isunmọ aṣoju tionkojalo labẹ atunda concurrency giga ni iyara laisi piparẹ-ikuna-yara fun awọn aṣoju ti o ku nitootọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_ENABLED`                            | `true`                   | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                          | Ṣeto `false` lati mu oluṣeto ilera aṣoju abẹlẹ kuro ti o ṣe iwadii awọn aṣoju ti o forukọsilẹ lorekore.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_INTERVAL_MS`                        | `600000`                 | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                          | Àárí ìgbálé ìlera ìpilẹ̀ṣẹ̀ olùṣàkóso nínú ms ( `60000` tó kéré jù).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_RELAY_INTERVAL_MS`                  | `21600000`               | `src/lib/proxyHealth/relayCadence.ts`                                                       | Àárín àkókò ìwádìí nínú ms fún àwọn aṣojú-ojú-igbẹ́ (`deno`/`vercel`/`cloudflare`), tí àwọn ìwádìí rẹ̀ jí àdádó aláìṣiṣẹ́mọ́ ( `60000` tó kéré jù).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_RECOVERY_INTERVAL_MS`               | `600000`                 | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                          | Aarin igbapada-kọja abẹlẹ ni ms: melo ni awọn aṣoju ti ko ni ilera tẹlẹ ti wa ni atunyẹwo nitoribẹẹ aṣoju ti o gba pada si yiyi laisi atunbere. Awọn iye ni isalẹ `60000` ṣubu pada si aiyipada.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_URL`                           | `https://httpbin.org/ip` | `src/lib/proxyHealth/probeTarget.ts`                                                        | Ibi-afẹde wiwa arọwọto ti a lo nipasẹ oluṣeto ati aaye ipari `/api/settings/proxies/auto-test`.
