| `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_CONCURRENCY`                   | `10`                     | `src/lib/proxyHealth/probeTarget.ts`                                                        | Awọn iwadii bẹrẹ ni ẹẹkan fun ipele kan, pinpin nipasẹ oluṣeto ati aaye ipari `/api/settings/proxies/auto-test`. Pakà ni 1 ati pe o wa ni 50.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_STAGGER_MS`                    | `100`                    | `src/lib/proxyHealth/probeTarget.ts`                                                        | Idaduro ni ms laarin awọn ilọkuro iwadii meji inu ipele kan. Laisi rẹ gbogbo ipele fi silẹ ni akoko kanna ati pinpin egress IP le ja ibi-afẹde opin-oṣuwọn kan. Ṣeto si `0` lati mu aye kuro; ti pari ni 5000.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_USE_PROVIDER_TARGET`                | `true`                   | `src/lib/proxyHealth/providerProbeTarget.ts`                                                | Ṣeto “eke” lati dẹkun ṣiṣewadii agbalejo gidi ti olupese ti a yàn ti aṣoju (`GET /models`, ko si bọtini API ) ati nigbagbogbo lo `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_URL` dipo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_AUTO_DEACTIVATE`                    | `false`                  | `src/lib/proxyHealth/statusPolicy.ts`                                                       | Nigbati `false` (aiyipada), awọn iwadii wiwa arọwọto adaṣe (oluṣeto + bọtini `/api/settings/proxies/auto-test` “Idanwo Gbogbo”) jẹ** kika-nikan** ati ki o ma ṣe kọ ipo aṣoju — nikan ni oniṣẹ ṣeto active/inactive, nitoribẹẹ iwadii alaburuku ko le fa aṣoju ti a yàn (#6246). Ṣeto `true` lati mu pada idanwo-jogunba-ati-ṣeto ihuwasi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `FLUSH_EMPTY_RETRY_ENABLED`                       | `false`                  | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                          | Jade-ni ẹya asia (wo [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md) ; a dasibodu DB bori bori). `true` (tabi `1`, `yes`) tun ṣe atunwo ṣiṣan ti o ṣofo ti yipada nipasẹ ọna ijẹrisi deede (to awọn igbiyanju `STREAM_RECOVERY.EMPTY_TURN_RETRY_MAX` ) dipo ṣiṣafihan 200 ofo tabi akoonu ofo 502.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `EMPTY_STREAM_SIBLING_RECOVERY_ENABLED`           | `false`                  | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                          | Jade-ni ẹya asia (wo [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md) ; a dasibodu DB bori bori). `true` (tabi `1`, `yes`) fi ọwọ si Claude/Codex ṣiṣan akoonu ofo ati ki o lo opin opin ti o wa tẹlẹ gbiyanju lori asopọ miiran ti o yẹ lẹhin titan `empty_content` kan, laisi itutu tabi titiipa awoṣe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `STREAM_LATE_OUTPUT_PROBE_ENABLED`                | `false`                  | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                          | Jade-ni ẹya asia (wo [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md) ; a dasibodu DB bori bori). `true` (tabi `1`, `yes`) ntọju iṣapẹẹrẹ ọkan ti a firanṣẹ siwaju ni 16 lẹhin akọkọ 64 ti o pẹ ti o han ni ṣiṣamisi ṣiṣan bi o ti njadejade (jade lẹhin-jade nikan 5xx duro ibeere-scoped dipo titiipa awoṣe-nikan).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `PROXY_POOL_EGRESS_OBSERVATION`                   | `false`                  | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                          | Jade-ni ẹya asia (wo [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md) ; a dasibodu DB bori bori). `true` (tabi `1`, `yes`) ṣe afihan akiyesi kika-nikan adagun egress labẹ adagun aṣoju ninu dasibodu (egress IPs ti o yatọ, awọn asopọ ati eyiti a rii julọ lẹhin IP kan lori wakati 24 to kẹhin, lati iwe aṣoju). Ko lo fun afisona.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `PROXY_OPERATOR_EGRESS_ENABLED`                   | `false`                  | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                          | Jade-ni ẹya asia (wo [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md) ; a dasibodu DB bori bori). `true` (tabi `1`, `yes`) gba oniṣẹ-titari dati awọn adirẹsi ti a ṣe akiyesi fun ọmọ ẹgbẹ adagun kan ati pe o da wọn pọ pẹlu iwe-akọọlẹ kika fun ifihan ati aṣẹ adagun-odo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `PROXY_OPERATOR_EGRESS_WINDOW_HOURS`              | `24`                     | `src/lib/db/proxyOperatorEgress.ts`                                                         | Ferese akiyesi ni awọn wakati fun awọn ori ila egress ti ti oniṣẹ ẹrọ (ka lori gbogbo kika; ko si atunbẹrẹ nilo). Ti a dè si 1..168; jade-ti-ibiti o, ti kii-nọmba, tabi ofo ṣubu pada si 24 pẹlu kan nikan ilana-jakejado ìkìlọ, kò jabọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE`                               | `false`                  | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                          | Ṣeto `true` lati jẹ ki oluṣeto gbe awọn aṣoju kuro ni adaṣe lẹhin awọn ikuna itẹlera leralera.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE_AFTER`                         | `3`                      | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                          | Awọn ikuna itẹlera ṣaaju ki oluṣeto afọwọṣe yọ aṣoju kuro (nigbati `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE=true`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `PROXY_AUTO_DISABLE`                              | `false`                  | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                          | Ṣeto `true` lati jẹ ki oluṣeto rọra-mu (ipo `dead`, ko paarẹ) aṣoju kan lẹhin awọn ikuna itẹlera leralera, dipo yiyọ kuro. Yiyan ti kii ṣe iparun si `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE`: aṣoju lọ silẹ ni ipinnu pool/rotation lẹsẹkẹsẹ (àlẹmọ ipo laaye ti a lo nipasẹ ipinnu adagun-omi ti o ti yọkuro tẹlẹ) ati pe a tun mu ṣiṣẹ laifọwọyi ni kete ti o bẹrẹ awọn iwadii lẹẹkansii. Ṣe alabapin ala `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE_AFTER`. Ti awọn asia mejeeji ba jẹ `true`, `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE` bori.                                                                                      |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_PASSIVE_SKIP`                       | `false`                  | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                          | Jade-in palolo skip: nigba ti `true`, awọn scheduler skips awọn ifiwe iwadi fun ohun endpoint ti laipe gbóògì ijabọ tẹlẹ mule o (ni o kere kan aseyori ko si si ikalara aṣoju ikuna ni awọn window, pato kan olupese woye). Paa nipa aiyipada: gbogbo aṣoju jẹ iwadii.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `PROXY_PASSIVE_WINDOW_MS`                         | `300000`                 | `src/lib/proxyHealth/passiveVerdict.ts`                                                     | Ferese ni ms ti iṣelọpọ ijabọ awọn palolo skip wulẹ ni (bounded `60000..599999`). Kukuru ju aarin igba gbigba iṣẹju mẹwa 10 nitoribẹẹ ikuna aipẹ kan jẹ gaba lori dipo ki a fomi po.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTROL_PLANE_PROXY_DIRECT_FALLBACK`   | `false`                  | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`                                            | Gba OAuth ati awọn ṣiṣan afọwọsi olupese lati fori aṣoju pinni ati sopọ taara nigbati awọn sọwedowo iṣaaju arọwọto aṣoju kuna. Itọkasi imunadoko ni Awọn asia Ẹya DB danu > env var > aiyipada.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `RATE_LIMIT_MAX_WAIT_MS`                          | `30000` (30s)            | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts`                                                            | Aiyipada** isinyi-duro** isuna: bi o gun a ìbéèrè le duro fun Iho olupese ati ki o joko ti isinyi ṣaaju ki o to ti o ti kọ, kò nínàgà awọn oke. Aago naa ti nu ni kete ti iṣẹ naa ba bẹrẹ sisẹ ṣiṣe — ti wa ni didi lọtọ nipasẹ `RATE_LIMIT_EXECUTION_MAX_WAIT_MS`. Env ṣeto aiyipada nikan: `resilienceSettings.requestQueue.maxWaitMs` ti o tẹsiwaju, ati lẹhinna asopọ kan `rateLimitOverrides.maxWaitMs`, kọọkan ṣẹgun rẹ.                                                                                                                                       |
| `RATE_LIMIT_EXECUTION_MAX_WAIT_MS`                | `600000` (iṣẹju 10)      | `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`                                                     | Aja fun igba melo ti ibeere ti o gba wọle le duro ni ipaniyan ṣaaju ki ifiṣura iye-oṣuwọn rẹ dopin — yọkuro lati inu isuna-iduro-duro nitoribẹẹ ibẹrẹ-lọra lori awọn ẹnu-ọna ti kii ṣe afikun ko ni akoko (#12027).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `RATE_LIMIT_MAX_QUEUE_DEPTH`                      | `0` (alaabo)             | `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`                                                     | Fila gbigba ti isinyi: kọ pẹlu 429 `queue_full` ni kete ti ọpọlọpọ awọn ibeere ti wa ni isinyi tẹlẹ. `0` = aipin (aiyipada).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `RATE_LIMIT_AUTO_ENABLE`                          | _(a ko ṣeto)_            | `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`                                                     | Fi agbara mu afọwọṣe agbara iye iwọn ailewu nẹtiwọki on/off laibikita eto Dashboard ti o duro (`resilienceSettings.requestQueue.autoEnableApiKeyProviders`). Gba `true`/`1`/`on` lati fi ipa mu, `false`/`0`/`off` lati fi ipa mu. Nigbati o ba n ṣiṣẹ (aiyipada), awọn asopọ bọtini API ti nṣiṣe lọwọ jẹ aabo oṣuwọn-iwọn laifọwọyi. Ṣiṣeto fun-asopọ `rateLimitProtection: false` mu aabo to fojuhan ṣiṣẹ ṣugbọn ko jade kuro ni nẹtiwọki aabo yii; Eto env var yii si `false` jẹ ilana agbaye lati ṣiṣe awọn asopọ bọtini API laisi awọn isinyi-iwọn-iye.         |
| `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_ENABLED`                       | _(kuro → kuro)_          | `open-sse/services/providerCooldownTracker.ts`                                              | Jade ni agbaye agbelebu-ibeere provider/connection cooldown titele. OFF nipasẹ aiyipada (ṣe agbekọja Asopọ Cooldown / Olupese Circuit Fifọ). Gba `true`/`1`/`on` lati mu ṣiṣẹ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_MIN_MS`                        | `5000`                   | `open-sse/services/providerCooldownTracker.ts`                                              | Itusilẹ ti o kere ju (ms) ṣaaju ki o to gbiyanju provider/connection ti o kuna. Ti ṣe iwọn ni iwọn pẹlu awọn ikuna itẹlera. Nikan lo nigbati `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_ENABLED`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_MAX_MS`                        | `300000` (5 min)         | `open-sse/services/providerCooldownTracker.ts`                                              | Fila itutu agbaiye (ms) ti o pọju ṣaaju ki o to gbiyanju provider/connection ti o kuna laiwo. Nikan lo nigbati `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_ENABLED`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED`                         | _(kuro → kuro)_          | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts` (irugbin) → `open-sse/services/streamRecovery.ts` (logbon) | **Kini:** sihin imularada ti truncated oke ṣiṣan (free-claude-koodu ibudo). Di ferese SSE šiši soke si `STREAM_RECOVERY.HOLDBACK_MS` (750 ms) nitorina _pre-commit_ cutoff — ọkan ti o ṣẹlẹ ṣaaju ki eyikeyi baiti de ọdọ alabara — ti wa ni ṣiṣi ati tun gbiyanju lairi.** Nigbawo lati mu ṣiṣẹ:** flaky/upstreams pe nigbagbogbo 0-baiti-truncate ni ibẹrẹ ṣiṣan; fi OFF silẹ ti o ko ba le ni anfani to 750 ms ti akoko afikun-si-akọkọ-aami lori gbogbo ṣiṣan. Gba `true`/`1`/`on`. Awọn irugbin eto Resilience ti o duro; eto Dasibodu AamiEye ni kete ti ṣeto. |
| `STREAM_RECOVERY_MIDSTREAM_ENABLED`               | _(kuro → kuro)_          | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts` (irugbin) → `open-sse/services/streamRecovery.ts` (logbon) | **Kini:** itesiwaju ṣiṣan aarin (Fase 4.4) — lẹhin _post-commit_ truncation (awọn baiti ti de ọdọ alabara tẹlẹ), tun beere pẹlu ọrọ apa kan gẹgẹbi iṣaju oluranlọwọ ati ki o di suffix ti o padanu. Ọrọ pẹtẹlẹ OpenAI-ibaramu awọn ṣiṣan nikan; ko ina pẹlu ipe irinṣẹ ni flight.** Nigbawo lati mu ṣiṣẹ:** awọn iran ti o gun ti o ge ni aarin-idahun ati pe o gba iru ti o gba pada ti o de bi ọkan ti nwaye kuku ju ami-nipasẹ-ami. Ominira ti `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED` (profaili eewu ti o yatọ). Gba `true`/`1`/`on`.                                          |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_ENABLED`              | _(kuro → kuro)_          | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts` → `open-sse/services/throughputWatchdog.ts`                | Jade-si-ṣiṣẹ-ṣiṣẹ wulo-jade oluṣọ. Ṣe awari awọn ṣiṣan ti o tọju fifiranṣẹ awọn chunks ṣugbọn wa ni isalẹ oṣuwọn atunto oluranlọwọ-jade; awọn ikọlu ọkan, awọn iṣẹlẹ lilo, deltas ofo, ati awọn ipele tool/reasoning ko ṣe ararẹ bi ilọsiwaju. Yatọ si aiṣiṣẹ ati awọn akoko ipari-lile. Awọn irugbin aiyipada nikan: Boolean ti a fipamọ sinu `resilienceSettings` (Dashboard → Settings → Resilience) gba iṣaaju.                                                                                                                                                  |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_WARMUP_MS`            | `30000`                  | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                  | Akoko oore-ọfẹ ṣaaju igbelewọn igbejade, ti a fi si 0–600000 ms.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_WINDOW_MS`            | `30000`                  | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                  | Yiyi ferese ti o wulo, ti a fi si 1000–600000 ms; window pipe kan nilo ṣaaju iṣẹyun.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_MIN_BYTES_PER_SECOND` | `4`                      | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                  | Oṣuwọn baiti oluranlọwọ-jade UTF-8 o kere ju (aṣoju àmi Konsafetifu), ti a dè si 1–1000000.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_MIN_USEFUL_BYTES`     | `1`                      | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                  | Ayẹwo abajade ti o wulo ti kii-odo ti o kere julọ ti a ro pe o le ṣewọn, ti a diwọn si 1–1000000 awọn baiti.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `HEALTHCHECK_STAGGER_MS`                          | `3000`                   | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                               | Stagger aarin (ms) laarin awọn sọwedowo ilera ami olupese ni ibẹrẹ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `HEALTHCHECK_JITTER_MIN_MS`                       | `500`                    | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                               | Jitter aileto ti o kere ju (ms) ti a ṣafikun lori oke `HEALTHCHECK_STAGGER_MS` laarin awọn sọwedowo ilera ami olupese, lati yago fun ti nwaye (Iran #1220).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `HEALTHCHECK_JITTER_MAX_MS`                       | `5000`                   | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                               | O pọju jitter aileto (ms) ti a ṣafikun lori oke `HEALTHCHECK_STAGGER_MS` laarin awọn sọwedowo ilera ti olupese, lati ṣe idiwọ ti nwaye (Iran #1220).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `HEALTHCHECK_BATCH_SIZE`                          | `20`                     | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                               | Ṣayẹwo-iwọn ipele nigbakanna fun gbigba ami-iṣayẹwo ibẹrẹ; awọn iye ti o tobi julọ ṣayẹwo awọn asopọ diẹ sii ni afiwe, awọn iye ti o kere julọ dinku fifuye ti nwaye (Iran # 7875, atunṣe ti # 7719).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `REQUEST_RETRY`                                   | `2`                      | `src/sse/services/cooldownAwareRetry.ts`                                                    | Nọmba awọn igbiyanju aifọwọyi lori awọn idahun itutu agbaiye awoṣe dopin ṣaaju ki o to pada aṣiṣe si alabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `MAX_RETRY_INTERVAL_SEC`                          | `30`                     | `src/sse/services/cooldownAwareRetry.ts`                                                    | Aarin ẹhin ti o pọju (awọn iṣẹju-aaya) laarin awọn igbiyanju itutu agbaiye. Capped nipasẹ iye yii laibikita `Retry-After` oke .                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `HEADROOM_URL`                                    | `http://localhost:8787`  | `src/lib/headroom/detect.ts`                                                                | Headroom tokini-ipamọ aṣoju URL. Igbesi aye dasibodu (`api/headroom/*`) nfa CLI `headroom-ai` agbegbe kan lori loopback nipasẹ aiyipada; idojuk nikan lati tọka si aṣoju ẹgbẹ ẹgbẹ Docker kan.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

### Ṣiṣan-imularada tuning ibakan

Ihuwasi idaduro imularada ti wa ni aifwy nipasẹ awọn alakọdi koodu ni `open-sse/config/constants.ts` (`STREAM_RECOVERY`), ti o han nibi fun itọkasi — yiyipada wọn nilo atunṣe koodu, kii ṣe env var:

- `STREAM_RECOVERY.HOLDBACK_MS = 750` — bi o ṣe pẹ to ti window SSE ṣiṣi yoo waye nitoribẹẹ truncation ni kutukutu ni a le tun gbiyanju ṣaaju ki eyikeyi baiti ṣe adehun si alabara.
- `STREAM_RECOVERY.BUFFER_MAX_BYTES = 65536` — fila lile lori ferese ti o waye; ṣe (fifọ + passthrough) ni kete ti ọpọlọpọ awọn baiti kojọpọ, laibikita aago naa.
- `STREAM_RECOVERY.EARLY_RETRY_MAX = 4` — max ṣiṣatunṣe ṣiṣafihan ṣiṣafihan ṣiṣan ti oke nigba ti idaduro idaduro ṣi ko ni ifaramọ.

> **Opin oṣuwọn sisun-window olupese (ko si env var):** FCC-ported fun-olupese sliding-window rate-limit _fallback_ wa ninu koodu (`open-sse/services/providerDefaultRateLimit.ts`, ti firanṣẹ nipasẹ `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`) ṣugbọn awọn ọkọ oju omi pẹlu ẹya** ṣofo map aiyipada** o si ni** ko si oniṣẹ env var** loni — o ti ṣiṣẹ nikan nipasẹ kio idanwo / satunkọ koodu. O ti wa ni imomose ko akojọ si ni awọn tabili loke. Awọn per-`(token, IP)` relay limiter ti _does_ ni koko ni `RELAY_IP_PER_MINUTE` (§3 Network & Ports).

| `GITHUB_ISSUES_TOKEN` | _(a ko ṣeto)_ | `src/app/api/v1/issues/report/route.ts`     | GitHub Àmi Wiwọle Ti ara ẹni pẹlu aaye `issues:write`.                                                                                                 |
| `GITHUB_TOKEN`        | _(a ko ṣeto)_ | oro triage / awọsanma oluranlowo oluranlọwọ | Jeneriki àmi iraye si GitHub ti a lo bi ipadasẹhin fun `GITHUB_ISSUES_TOKEN` ati jẹun nipasẹ awọn oluranlọwọ aṣoju awọsanma ni `src/lib/cloudAgent/*`. |

## Mu Free-Tier Jeki-Laye

Ṣe spins iṣẹ wẹẹbu ọfẹ ni isalẹ lẹhin awọn iṣẹju 15 laisi ijabọ inbound. Ifilọlẹ imurasilẹ (`scripts/dev/run-standalone.mjs`, eyiti aworan Docker nṣiṣẹ) le ping URL ti gbogbo eniyan ti iṣẹ lati ṣe idiwọ yẹn. Ko ṣe nkankan ayafi ti URL ti gbogbo eniyan ba wa, ati pe o wọle nikan nigbati ipo ba yipada.

| Ayípadà                      | Aiyipada                 | Apejuwe                                                                                                                 |
| ---------------------------- | ------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `SELF_PING_ENABLED`          | lori nigbati a ṣeto URL  | `0`, `false`, `off` or `no` turns the pinger off. Use it on paid Render plans, where nothing spins down.                |
| `SELF_PING_URL`              | `RENDER_EXTERNAL_URL`    | Ipilẹ URL si ping. Ṣeto ṣeto `RENDER_EXTERNAL_URL` funrararẹ lori gbogbo ero, nitorinaa pinger wa nibẹ nipasẹ aiyipada. |
| `SELF_PING_PATH`             | `/api/monitoring/health` | Ona lori URL yẹn. Gbọdọ bẹrẹ pẹlu `/`; ohunkohun miiran disables awọn pinger pẹlu kan Ikilọ.                            |
| `SELF_PING_INTERVAL_SECONDS` | `180`                    | Awọn aaya laarin pings, lẹhin ping akọkọ 60 aaya in. Awọn iye ni isalẹ `30` ṣubu pada si `180`.                         |

Iṣẹ kan ti o ṣọna fun oṣu kan lo bii awọn wakati apẹẹrẹ 744, nitorinaa ṣayẹwo rẹ lodi si iyọọda ipele ọfẹ lọwọlọwọ ti Render.

## Àwọn Àpẹẹrẹ Ìgbékalẹ̀

Fún ìtọ́sọ́nà SRE backend relay (ìhùwàsí ts/bifrost/auto, ibi tí 9router àti CLIProxyAPI yẹ kí wọ́n wà, àti ọgbọ́n fallback fún ìgbésókè throughput), wo [Ọgbọ́n Backend Relay](/docs/reference/RELAY_BACKEND_STRATEGY.md).

### Ìdàgbàsókè Agbègbè Kéré Jù

```bash
JWT_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 48)
API_KEY_SECRET=$(openssl rand -hex 32)
INITIAL_PASSWORD=dev123
PORT=20128
NODE_ENV=development
```

### Ìṣelọpọ Docker

```bash
JWT_SECRET=<generated>
API_KEY_SECRET=<generated>
INITIAL_PASSWORD=<generated>
STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY=<generated>
DATA_DIR=/data
PORT=20128
API_PORT=20129
NODE_ENV=production
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true
NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL=https://omniroute.example.com
BASE_URL=http://localhost:20128
OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB=8192
CORS_ORIGIN=https://your-frontend.example.com
```

### Air-Gapped / CI

```bash
JWT_SECRET=test-jwt-secret-for-ci
API_KEY_SECRET=test-api-key-secret-for-ci
INITIAL_PASSWORD=testpass
NODE_ENV=production
OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_BACKGROUND_SERVICES=true
APP_LOG_TO_FILE=false
```

### VPS pẹ̀lú Reverse Proxy (nginx + Cloudflare)

```bash
JWT_SECRET=<generated>
API_KEY_SECRET=<generated>
STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY=<generated>
PORT=20128
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true
NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL=https://omniroute.example.com
BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:20128
CORS_ORIGIN=https://omniroute.example.com
ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT=true
CLI_COMPAT_ALL=1
```

---

## 24. Apoti Iyanrin ogbon (v3.8.0 +)

Awọn ifilelẹ lọ ati awọn bọtini aabo ti a lo nigbati ilana Awọn ogbon (`src/lib/skills/`) nṣiṣẹ awọn adaṣe asọye olumulo ni agbegbe iyanrin.

| Ayípadà                           | Aiyipada                                      | Faili orisun                                                       | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| --------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS`       | `10000` (iṣẹju 10)                            | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Akoko akoko odi-ipaniyan fun awọn koodu olorijori apoti iyanrin. Fila lile; ohunkohun to gun ti wa ni pa.                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `SKILLS_EXECUTION_TIMEOUT_MS`     | _(ṣubu pada si `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS`) _ | `src/lib/skills/`                                                  | Ipele-giga olorijori orchestration akoko ti pari. Ṣeto ti o ga ju `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS` lati gba ṣiṣan iṣẹ-igbesẹ lọpọlọpọ.                                                                                                                                                        |
| `SKILLS_MAX_FILE_BYTES`           | `1048576` (1 MB)                              | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Awọn baiti ti o pọju ọgbọn le ka lati eyikeyi faili ti o ni apoti iyanrin.                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `SKILLS_MAX_HTTP_RESPONSE_BYTES`  | `256000` (250 KB)                             | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Max awọn baiti sile lati eyikeyi nikan HTTP esi inu kan olorijori.                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `SKILLS_MAX_SANDBOX_OUTPUT_CHARS` | `100000`                                      | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Fila lile lori awọn ohun kikọ stdout/stderr pada lati epe apoti iyanrin kan.                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_NETWORK_ENABLED`  | `false`                                       | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Ṣeto `1`/`true` lati gba nẹtiwọki laaye lati inu apoti iyanrin. Awọn aiyipada si** ya sọtọ** fun ailewu.                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `SKILLS_ALLOWED_SANDBOX_IMAGES`   | _(sofo)_                                      | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Akojọ iyọọda ti o ya sọtọ komama ti awọn aworan apoti idasilẹ fun ipaniyan apoti iyanrin. Ofo tumọ si aiyipada ti a ṣe sinu nikan.                                                                                                                                                       |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_DOCKER_IMAGE`     | _(aiyipada ti a ṣe sinu)_                     | `src/lib/skills/`                                                  | Aworan apoti ti a lo nigbati o ba n tan apoti iyanrin ti o ṣe atilẹyin Docker. Daju lati pin aworan ipilẹ ti o ni lile ti aṣa.                                                                                                                                                           |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_RUNTIME`          | `auto`                                        | `src/lib/skills/sandbox.ts`, `src/lib/skills/containerProvider.ts` | Aago asiko apoti fun olorijori apoti: `auto` \| `docker` \| `apple` \| `wsl` \| `orbstack` \| `podman`. `auto` n yan akoko asiko ti o dara julọ ti a fi sori ẹrọ fun OS agbalejo (Apple Container/OrbStack lori macOS, apoti WSL lori Windows, Podman lori Linux), ja bo pada si Docker. |

> [!CAUTION]
> Muu `SKILLS_SANDBOX_NETWORK_ENABLED=true` ṣiṣẹ ṣi ọna egress lati koodu ọgbọn lainidii. Papọ pẹlu `OUTBOUND_SSRF_GUARD_ENABLED=true` ati eto imulo `CORS_ORIGIN`/aṣoju ti o muna ni awọn imuṣiṣẹ pinpin.

## 25. Àwọn Quota Provider, Àwọn Tunnel, Àwọn Afẹ́yinti & Àwọn Miíràn Runtime

Ìfagilé fallback ìbámu-session Redis (opt-in nìkan; àiyipada SQLite kò yí padà):

Àwọn store ìbámu Redis kọ̀ọ̀kan gba àwọn iṣẹ́ tí kò tíì parí tó 8 péré; nígbà tí agbára bá kún, àwọn ìpè máa lo SQLite lẹ́sẹ̀kẹsẹ̀, àwọn slot sì máa wà ní ipò tí wọ́n ti gba títí iṣẹ́ Redis tó wà lábẹ́ rẹ̀ yóò fi yanju, kódà lẹ́yìn deadline 100 ms ẹni tó pè.

- R1. Gbogbo ìdáhùn Redis tí a bá fi dé ní àkókò fún iṣẹ́ session jẹ́ aláṣẹ: àwọn ìyípadà tí a gbà, CAS tí a kọ tàbí tí ó sọnù, àwọn kọ́kọ́rọ́ tí kò sí, àwọn ìparẹ́ kọ́kọ́rọ́ tí kò sí, àti àwọn ìṣẹ̀lẹ̀ eviction tí ó bá kọ́ tàbí tí kò bá kọ́, gbogbo wọn máa ń pa row fallback àdúgbò session yẹn rẹ̀ láìṣiṣẹ́pọ̀, láìsí await kankan láàárín ìdáhùn àti ìparẹ́. Ìparẹ́ jẹ́ àìléwu (ní búburú jù, yíyan padà kan lè ṣẹlẹ̀ nígbà outage tó kàn); row tó ti di àtijọ́ kì í ṣe bẹ́ẹ̀.
- R2. Iṣẹ́ kan tí ó bá kọjá àkókò tàbí tí ó bá ní aṣiṣe tí ó sì kọ fallback àdúgbò máa ń fi ohun tí ó kọ sílẹ̀ gẹ́gẹ́ bí bytes tí a serialize. Bí àṣẹ Redis rẹ̀ bá parí ní pẹ́, SQLite compare-and-delete tó ń ṣiṣẹ́pọ̀ máa ń pa row tó bá jẹ́ byte-for-byte pẹ̀lú rẹ̀ nìkan, kì í ṣe pẹ̀lú ìbámu account tàbí connection. Ìkọ̀ outage tuntun máa ń là á já.
- R3. Eviction máa ń yọ pin àdúgbò account tí ó kuna kúrò ní gbogbo ìgbà, pẹ̀lú nígbà outage; ìmúlò ìdáhùn rẹ̀ yóò tẹ̀lé R1/R2 lẹ́yìn náà. Redis tó ní ìlera kì í kọ pins àdúgbò: kò sí mirror tàbí ledger títọ́-sílẹ̀, àwọn instance sì lè yàtọ̀ sí ara wọn nígbà outage.

Àwọn endpoint quota provider, àwọn tunnel nẹ́tíwọ̀ọ̀kì (Tailscale, Ngrok, MITM debug proxy), adágún egress 1Proxy, àwọn afẹ́yinti database àti àwọn overrides kékeré fún feature kọ̀ọ̀kan tí layer executor tàbí àwọn script tọ́ka sí.

| Variable                                       | Default                                                                     | Source File                                                               | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ---------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_SESSION_AFFINITY_STORE`             | `sqlite`                                                                    | `src/lib/db/sessionAffinity/storeFactory.ts`                              | Ẹ̀yìn ìfaramọ́ láàárín session àti account. `redis` pẹ̀lú `REDIS_URL` tí a ti ṣètò nìkan ló máa ń mú kí pínpín ṣiṣẹ́; àwọn iye tí kò bá sí tàbí tí a kò mọ̀, tàbí àìsí ìṣètò Redis, máa ń lo SQLite. Ó nílò `sessionAffinityTtlMs` rere. Ìtọ́jú routing Redis máa ń fipamọ́ ìdánimọ̀ usage-account tí a ti hash pọ̀ mọ́ ID ìsopọ̀; routing SQLite àìyípadà máa ń pa irú JSON pápá mẹ́rin àtijọ́ mọ́. Ó máa ń bá ìdánimọ̀ mu nínú pool agbegbe tó yẹ kí ó tó lo ID. Ó ń lo `REDIS_KEY_PREFIX` oníbàárà pínpín, àwọn iye JSON àti TTL `PX`; àwọn ìyípadà máa ń fi iye gangan tí a rí wé. Àwọn ìdáhùn Redis tó ní ìlera máa ń sọ fallback ìdènà agbegbe di asán, àti pé ìdáhùn tó pẹ́ lè pa ìlà fallback tí a ṣe àkọsílẹ̀ rẹ̀ nìkan tí ó bá jẹ́ iye serialized gangan tí iṣẹ́ tó kọjá àkókò yẹn kọ. Àwọn pin tí a ṣàkọ́kọ́ mú wá tún máa ń jẹ́rìí sí nígbà lílo. Àwọn àṣìṣe Redis, àkókò tó kọjá tàbí kíkún àyè gbigba máa ń padà sí SQLite pẹ̀lú àwọn ìkìlọ̀ tí a dín ìgbà wọn kù (ààlà 100 ms fún ìpè store kọ̀ọ̀kan); àwọn Redis miss tó ṣàṣeyọrí kì í ka shadow. Kò pín ìdúróṣinṣin combo, àwọn pin ìyípadà Codex abinibi, ìdíje account, àwọn ìdènà tàbí àwọn ààlà ináwó. |
| `REDIS_URL`                                    | `redis://localhost:6379`                                                    | `src/shared/utils/rateLimiter.ts`                                         | Ọ̀nà ìsopọ̀ Redis fún ẹ̀yìn rate limiter.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `ALIBABA_CODING_PLAN_HOST`                     | _(host production)_                                                         | `open-sse/services/bailianQuotaFetcher.ts`                                | Yí host tí a máa lò láti gba àwọn quota Alibaba Bailian coding-plan padà.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `ALIBABA_CODING_PLAN_QUOTA_URL`                | derived from host                                                           | `open-sse/services/bailianQuotaFetcher.ts`                                | Àyípadà URL quota kíkún fún Alibaba Bailian.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `QWEN_CLOUD_COOKIE`                            | _(unset)_                                                                   | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | Kúkì session console fún ẹnu-ọ̀nà quota Token Plan ti ara ẹni Qwen Cloud / Model Studio (API key inference kò lè ka a). Da gbogbo `Cookie` request header kọ́ — ó ní `login_qwencloud_ticket` nínú — láti inú ìpè `api.json` èyíkéyìí sí `cs-data.qwencloud.com` lórí home.qwencloud.com › Billing › Subscription (F12 › Network). Ó ní ìpamọ́, ó sì ní ààlà session; ó dára kí a lo pápá Dashboard `qwenCloudCookie` fún ìsopọ̀ kọ̀ọ̀kan.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `QWEN_CLOUD_SEC_TOKEN`                         | _(unset)_                                                                   | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | Àyípadà afọwọ́kọ `sec_token` fún ẹnu-ọ̀nà console Token Plan. Ó ní ìpamọ́; nígbà tí kò bá sí, fetcher máa ń yọ ọ́ jáde láti inú HTML dashboard nípasẹ̀ kúkì náà.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `QWEN_TOKEN_PLAN_HOST`                         | `https://cs-data.qwencloud.com`                                             | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | Àṣàyípadà host ẹnu-ọ̀nà fún olùgbà quota Token Plan ti ara ẹni (fún àpẹẹrẹ, `bailian-singapore-cs.alibabacloud.com` fún console Model Studio).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `QWEN_TOKEN_PLAN_DASHBOARD_URL`                | `https://home.qwencloud.com/`                                               | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | URL Dashboard tí a ń lò láti yọ `sec_token` kúrò nínú HTML ẹni tí ó ti wọlé.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `XIAOMI_MIMO_CONSOLE_COOKIE`                   | _(kò ṣètò)_                                                                 | `open-sse/services/usage/xiaomi-mimo.ts`                                  | Kúkì ìgbà ìpàdé console fún API quota Xiaomi MiMo Token Plan (`platform.xiaomimimo.com/api/v1/tokenPlan/usage` + `tokenPlan/detail` — kókó API inference kò lè kà á). Da gbogbo akọsórí ìbéèrè `Cookie` náà kọ — ó ní `api-platform_serviceToken` àti `userId` nínú — láti inú ìbéèrè èyíkéyìí sí `platform.xiaomimimo.com` lórí ojú-ìwé Token Plan (F12 › Network). Ó ní ìmọ̀ aṣírí, ó sì ní ààlà sí ìgbà ìpàdé; ó dára kí o lo pápá Dashboard `xiaomiMimoConsoleCookie` fún ìsopọ̀ kọ̀ọ̀kan.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `XIAOMI_MIMO_MONTHLY_TOKEN_LIMIT`              | `4100000000`                                                                | `open-sse/services/usage/xiaomi-mimo.ts`                                  | Isuna oṣù (Credits) fún fallback Xiaomi MiMo tí OmniRoute ń tọ́pinpin, tí a ń lò nígbà tí a kò bá ṣètò kúkì console. Àìpẹ́yà rẹ̀ jẹ́ 4.1B ti ipele Lite; ṣètò rẹ̀ sí Credits ètò náà (Standard 11B / Pro 38B / Max 82B). Àṣàyípadà `providerSpecificData.monthlyTokenLimit` fún ìsopọ̀ kọ̀ọ̀kan máa borí oníyípadà ayíká yìí.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

Afárá máa ń jáde nígbà ìbẹ̀rẹ̀ bí àwọn fáìlì fún àwòṣe tí ń ṣiṣẹ́ bá sọnù.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

Ó máa ń dá àiyipada àkọ́kọ́ nìkan sílẹ̀: iye boolean tí a fipamọ́ sínú `resilienceSettings` (Dasibodu → Ètò → Ìfaradà) ni ó máa ní ààyò.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

| `RELAY_ROUTING_BACKEND`                        | _(kò ṣeto)_                                                                 | `src/app/api/v1/relay/chat/completions/routingBackend.ts`                 | Àpèjá tí a gbà fún `OMNIROUTE_RELAY_BACKEND` (àwọn iye `ts \| bifrost \| auto` kan náà). `OMNIROUTE_RELAY_BACKEND` ni ààyò nígbà tí a bá ṣètò méjèèjì.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

Pípèsè cert/key kan ṣoṣo, tàbí ọ̀nà tí a kò lè kà, máa ń kọ ìkìlọ̀, ó sì máa dúró lórí HTTP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

Ó gbọ́dọ̀ wà pẹ̀lú `OMNIROUTE_TLS_CERT`. Wo `OMNIROUTE_TLS_CERT`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

Nígbà tí a kò bá ṣètò rẹ̀ tàbí tí iye rẹ̀ bá jẹ́ `0`, gbogbo route `/api/local/*` máa dá `503` padà ní production. Ó gbọ́dọ̀ jẹ́ `1` nínú àwọn ìtẹ̀síwájú tí kò jẹ́ loopback láti lè ṣiṣẹ́ Redis launcher àti àwọn olùbẹ̀rẹ̀ iṣẹ́ local míì tí a lè bẹ̀rẹ̀ pẹ̀lú tẹ̀ kan. | `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_ENDPOINTS_TOKEN`              | _(kò ṣeto)_                                                                 | `src/lib/security/localEndpoints.ts`                                      | Tokeni Bearer fún àwọn olùpè `/api/local/*` tí kò wà lórí loopback (fún àpẹẹrẹ, app desktop). Ó jẹ́ dandan nígbà tí `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_ENDPOINTS_ENABLED=1` nínú àwọn ìtẹ̀síwájú tí kò jẹ́ loopback.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

Pọ́ọ̀tì inú àpótí náà ṣì máa jẹ́ 6379.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDIS_BIND_HOST`                    | `127.0.0.1`                                                                 | `bin/cli/commands/redis.mjs`                                              | Ojú-ọ̀nà host tí olùgbékalẹ̀ Redis títẹ̀kan ti ń tẹ̀jáde sí. Olùgbékalẹ̀ náà máa ń bẹ̀rẹ̀ Redis LAÍ Ọ̀RỌ̀-ÌGBANIWỌLÉ, nítorí náà dídi mọ́ `0.0.0.0` máa fún gbogbo host tó wà lórí LAN rẹ ní Redis tí kò ní ìfọwọ́sí — gbé ààlà yìí sókè nìkan bí o bá tún ṣètò ọ̀rọ̀-ìgbaniwọlé fún instance náà fúnra rẹ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `REDIS_BIND_HOST`                              | `127.0.0.1`                                                                 | `docker-compose.yml`                                                      | Ojú-ọ̀nà host tí docker-compose ti ń tẹ̀jáde Redis sidecar sí (#9286). Redis compose náà ń ṣiṣẹ́ láìsí `requirepass`; àwọn àpótí app máa ń dé ọ̀dọ̀ rẹ̀ lórí nẹ́tíwọ́ọ̀kì compose (`redis:6379`) — pọ́ọ̀tì tí a tẹ̀jáde wà fún àwọn irinṣẹ́ tó ń ṣiṣẹ́ ní ẹgbẹ́ host nìkan. `0.0.0.0` máa ń ṣí Redis tí kò ní ìfọwọ́sí sí gbogbo LAN.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `REDIS_PORT`                                   | `6379`                                                                      | `docker-compose.yml`                                                      | Pọ́ọ̀tì host fún Redis sidecar compose.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `APP_BIND_HOST`                                | `127.0.0.1`                                                                 | `docker-compose.yml`, `docker-compose.prod.yml`                           | Ojú-ọ̀nà host tí docker-compose ti ń tẹ̀jáde àwọn pọ́ọ̀tì dashboard/API/live-WS ti app fúnra rẹ̀ sí (#12568). Níwọ̀n bí `REQUIRE_API_KEY=false` ti jẹ́ àṣàyàn aiyipada nínú `.env.example`, `0.0.0.0` máa ń ṣí aṣojú LLM aláìlórúkọ `/v1` sí gbogbo LAN/WAN — gbé ààlà yìí sókè lẹ́yìn tí `REQUIRE_API_KEY=true` bá wà tàbí tí reverse proxy kan tó wà níwájú bá ń fipá mú ìfọwọ́sí tirẹ̀.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `QDRANT_BIND_HOST`                             | `127.0.0.1`                                                                 | `docker-compose.yml`                                                      | Ojú-ọ̀nà host tí docker-compose ti ń tẹ̀jáde Qdrant memory sidecar sí (#12578). Ìdí tó kan ìfihàn sí LAN jẹ́ bákan náà pẹ̀lú `REDIS_BIND_HOST`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `BIFROST_BIND_HOST`                            | `127.0.0.1`                                                                 | `docker-compose.yml`                                                      | Ojú-ọ̀nà host tí docker-compose ti ń tẹ̀jáde Bifrost router sidecar sí (#12578). Ìdí tó kan ìfihàn sí LAN jẹ́ bákan náà pẹ̀lú `REDIS_BIND_HOST`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `REDIS_KEY_PREFIX`                             | `omniroute:`                                                                | `src/shared/utils/rateLimiter.ts`                                         | Àfikún namespace tí a máa fi sí gbogbo kọ́kọ́rọ̀ OmniRoute Redis (olùdínà ìwọlé tó pọ̀jù, cache ìfàṣẹsí, ibi ìpamọ́ quota, àti circuit breaker ìmúgbóná). Ó dènà ìkọlù kọ́kọ́rọ̀ nígbà tí Redis instance bá ń bá àwọn app mìíràn pín (#11042).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_INTERNAL_SERVICE_TOKEN`             | _(unset — mechanism disabled)_                                              | `src/lib/api/internalServiceAuth.ts`                                      | Asiri tí a pín fún àwọn ìgbésẹ̀ REST inú iṣẹ́ tí ń pa ìdánimọ̀ mọ́ (#9260): àwọn paati OmniRoute tí ń pe àwọn route OmniRoute agbègbè mìíràn ń fi ránṣẹ́ gẹ́gẹ́ bí `x-omniroute-internal-service-token` kí ìdánimọ̀ olùpè ìpilẹ̀ṣẹ̀ lè wà láìyípadà. A fi wé `timingSafeEqual`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_INTERNAL_SERVICE_TOKEN_FILE`        | _(unset)_                                                                   | `src/lib/api/internalServiceAuth.ts`                                      | Àyípadà fáìlì-asiri ti token iṣẹ́ inú: ọ̀nà sí fáìlì kan tí àkóónú rẹ̀ tí a gé ààyè yí ká jẹ́ token náà. A máa kàn wò ó nígbà tí inline var kò bá ṣètò.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OPENROUTER_PROVIDER_STATS_ENABLED`            | `true`                                                                      | `src/lib/catalog/openrouterProviderStats.ts`                              | Fi àwọn iṣiro ìṣètò ọ̀sẹ̀ OpenRouter kún àtòjọ àwọn pèsè dashboard (#9324). Ó wà ní titan láìsí àgbékalẹ̀ mìíràn; ṣètò `false` láti fo ìgbéjáde abẹ́lẹ̀ náà kọjá pátápátá (kò dí ìṣiṣẹ́ dúró, kò sì lè fa aṣìṣe tó burú).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OPENROUTER_PROVIDER_STATS_TTL_MS`             | `86400000` (24h)                                                            | `src/lib/catalog/openrouterProviderStats.ts`                              | TTL cache fún àwòrán snapshot àwọn iṣiro pèsè OpenRouter, ní milliseconds.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDIS_IMAGE`                        | `redis:7-alpine`                                                            | `bin/cli/commands/redis.mjs`                                              | Àwòrán Redis tí olùgbékalẹ̀ Redis tí a tẹ̀ lẹ́ẹ̀kan máa ń lò. Yí i padà sí `redis:8-alpine` tàbí sí mirror registry aládàáni bí ó bá ṣe yẹ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
