Àiyipada tọ́ka sí orúkọ iṣẹ́ qdrant tó wà nínú nẹ́tíwọ̀ọ̀kì; yí i padà fún ìgbékalẹ̀ òde. A máa lò ó nìkan nígbà tí `qdrantEnabled` bá jẹ́ `true` nínú kóòdù (`src/lib/memory/vectorStore.ts:108`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `QDRANT_PORT`                                  | `6333`                                                                      | _(opt-in cluster profile)_                                                | Pọ́ọ̀tì REST ti sidecar Qdrant.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `QDRANT_GRPC_PORT`                             | `6334`                                                                      | _(àṣàyàn profaili cluster)_                                               | Pọ́ọ̀tì gRPC ti sidecar Qdrant. Àwọn ilé-ìkàwé client tí wọ́n fẹ́ràn gRPC ju REST lọ fún àwọn iṣẹ́ ṣiṣanwọlé ni ó máa ń lò ó.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `QDRANT_API_KEY`                               | _(unset)_                                                                   | _(àṣàyàn profaili cluster)_                                               | Bọtini API àṣàyàn fún Qdrant Cloud tàbí àpẹrẹ on-prem tí ó ní ìfọwọ́sí. Empty → a kò fi akọsori `api-key` ránṣẹ́.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `QDRANT_COLLECTION`                            | `omniroute-memory`                                                          | _(àṣàyàn profaili cluster)_                                               | Orúkọ àkójọpọ̀ fún àwọn embeddings ìrántí ìjíròrò OmniRoute. A máa ṣẹ̀dá a ní ìṣàkọ́kọ́ pẹ̀lú àwọn dimensions `QDRANT_VECTOR_SIZE`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |

Àwọn embeddings gidi ni provider èyíkéyìí tí pápá `embeddingModel` nínú àwọn ètò OmniRoute tọ́ka sí máa ń ṣe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `QDRANT_VECTOR_SIZE`                           | `1536`                                                                      | _(àṣàyàn profaili cluster)_                                               | Ìwọ̀n vector embedding. Ó gbọ́dọ̀ bá àwòṣe tí ẹ fi ń ṣe embedding mu (text-embedding-3-small → 1536; ada-002 → 1536; nomic-embed-text → 768).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `QDRANT_HNSW_EF_CONSTRUCT`                     | `128`                                                                       | _(àṣàyàn profaili cluster)_                                               | Ìpéye ní àkókò kíkọ́ atọ́ka HNSW. | `OMNIROUTE_ROTATION_ENABLED`                   | `true`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Àyípadà àgbà fún yíyí àkọọ́lẹ̀ tí oniṣẹ́ lè ṣàtúnṣe. Nígbà tí ó bá jẹ́ `false`, kò sí ọ̀kan nínú àwọn class `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_*` tó wà nísàlẹ̀ tí yóò mú account fallback ṣiṣẹ́ (ipò tí master bá wà ní pipa tún máa dí àwọn class 429/500/502 tí a gbà lágbára ní àiyípẹ́). Ó jẹ́ kí front-end alábòójútó (fún àpẹẹrẹ, app desktop VibeProxy) lè fi àwọn òfin yíyí tirẹ̀ hàn lórí ẹ́ńjìnnì account-fallback backend.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATION_RATE_LIMIT_RESET_SECONDS`  | `0`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Àkókò ìtútù (ìṣẹ́jú-aaya) tí a máa lò fún account tí a ti dá dúró nítorí rate limit nígbà tí upstream kò bá fún ní àfihàn ìtúnṣètò kankan. `0` = lo àkókò ìtútù àiyípẹ́ ẹ́ńjìnnì dípò override tí a ṣètò pẹ́ títí.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATION_DISABLE_TAG_WITHOUT_RESET` | `true`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Àfihàn ìfẹ́ràn iwájú-ẹ̀yìn “má ṣe samisi gẹ́gẹ́ bí ó ti dé ààlà ìbéèrè láìsí àkókò ìtúnṣe.”                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_429`                      | `true`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Ìmúṣiṣẹ́ àfidípò fún ipo `429`. Nígbà tí ó bá jẹ́ `false` (àti `OMNIROUTE_ROTATION_ENABLED=true`), `429` kò tún máa fa yíyí àkọọ́lẹ̀ padà, a sì máa dá a padà sí oníbàárà dípò.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_429_THRESHOLD`               | `1`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Iye àwọn àṣìṣe `429` tí ó gbọ́dọ̀ ṣẹlẹ̀ láàárín `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_429_WINDOW_SECONDS` kí a tó yí àkọọ́lẹ̀ padà. `1` (àìpẹ́) máa ń yí i padà lẹ́sẹ̀kẹsẹ̀, ó sì ń pa ìhùwàsí àtijọ́ mọ́.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_429_WINDOW_SECONDS`          | `120`                                                                       | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Àkókò yíyọ (ìṣẹ́jú-aaya) tí a máa ń ka àwọn àṣìṣe `429` sí nínú rẹ̀ láti bá `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_429_THRESHOLD` mu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_500`                      | `true`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Ìmúṣiṣẹ́ àfidípò fún ipo àwọn àṣìṣe server `5xx` (yàtọ̀ sí `502`, tí ó ní kíláàsì tirẹ̀). Nígbà tí ó bá jẹ́ `false`, àwọn àṣìṣe wọ̀nyí kò tún máa fa yíyí àkọọ́lẹ̀ padà.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_500_THRESHOLD`               | `1`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Iye àwọn àṣìṣe `5xx` tí ó gbọ́dọ̀ ṣẹlẹ̀ láàárín `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_500_WINDOW_SECONDS` kí a tó yí àkọọ́lẹ̀ padà. `1` (àìpẹ́) máa ń yí i padà lẹ́sẹ̀kẹsẹ̀.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_500_WINDOW_SECONDS`          | `120`                                                                       | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Àkókò yíyọ (ìṣẹ́jú-aaya) tí a máa ń ka àwọn àṣìṣe `5xx` sí nínú rẹ̀ láti bá `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_500_THRESHOLD` mu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_502`                      | `true`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Ìmúṣiṣẹ́ àfidípò fún àwọn àṣìṣe `502` (ẹnu-ọ̀nà búburú). Nígbà tí ó bá jẹ́ `false`, `502` kò tún máa fa yíyí àkọọ́lẹ̀ padà.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_THRESHOLD`               | `1`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Nọ́mbà àwọn aṣìṣe `502` nínú `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_WINDOW_SECONDS` tí a nílò kí a tó yí akọọlẹ padà. `1` (àìyípadà) máa ń yí i padà lẹ́sẹ̀kẹsẹ̀.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_WINDOW_SECONDS`          | `120`                                                                       | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Àkókò ìṣàkóso tí ń yí (ìṣẹ́jú-aaya) tí a máa ń ka àwọn aṣìṣe `502` sí nínú rẹ̀ fún `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_THRESHOLD`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_400`                      | `false`                                                                     | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Ìmúṣiṣẹ́ pẹ̀lú ìfẹ́ (àìṣiṣẹ́ ní àìyípadà): nígbà tí ó bá jẹ́ `true`, `400` lasán (ìbéèrè tí kò tọ́) náà máa ń fa yíyí akọọlẹ padà. Èyí jẹ́ àfikún nìkan — kò ṣe díẹ̀kù sí ìhùwàsí tó wà tẹ́lẹ̀ nínú engine, níbi tí `400` tí ó ní ọ̀rọ̀ ààlà oṣùwọ̀n/quota ṣì máa ń fa ìṣàn padà láìka àsíá yìí sí.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_THRESHOLD`               | `1`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Nọ́mbà àwọn aṣìṣe `400` nínú `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_WINDOW_SECONDS` tí a nílò kí a tó yí akọọlẹ padà (a máa ń kàn sí i nígbà tí `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_400=true` nìkan).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_WINDOW_SECONDS`          | `120`                                                                       | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Àkókò ìṣàkóso tí ń yí (ìṣẹ́jú-aaya) tí a máa ń ka àwọn aṣìṣe `400` sí nínú rẹ̀ fún `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_THRESHOLD`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

### Olùṣètò Ìgbóná-àkọ́kọ́ Claude

Ìgbóná-àkọ́kọ́ tí Cron ń darí fún àwọn ìsopọ̀ Anthropic OAuth tí a ti yàn, kí ferese ààlà oṣùwọ̀n wákàtí 5 lè ṣí pẹ̀lú ìbéèrè kékeré tí a ṣètò dípò ìbéèrè gidi àkọ́kọ́ (#8848). Olùṣètò náà wà ní pipa àfi tí `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_ENABLED` bá jẹ́ truthy **àti** tí a bá fi àsíá sí ìsopọ̀ náà nínú `settings.claudeWarmup.connections`; àkójọpọ̀ ìsopọ̀ tó ṣófo túmọ̀ sí pé a kò ní gbóná ìsopọ̀ kankan, kódà tí env var náà bá wà ní titan.

| Variable                       | Àìyípadà                    | Fáìlì Orísun                 | Àpèjúwe                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ------------------------------ | --------------------------- | ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_ENABLED`     | _(unset → off)_             | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Àtọ́ka àgbáyé fún olùṣètò ìgbóná-àkọ́kọ́. Ó gba `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` (kò ṣe pàtàkì bóyá lẹ́tà ńlá tàbí kékeré, a sì ń gé àlàfo kúrò). Iye míì, tàbí tí a kò bá ṣètò ọ́, máa ń jẹ́ kí olùṣètò náà wà ní pipa. |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_CRON`        | `0 7 * * *`                 | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Àpẹẹrẹ cron pápá márùn-ún fún ìgbà ìṣiṣẹ́ ìgbóná-àkọ́kọ́, tí a ń ṣe àyẹ̀wò rẹ̀ ní `America/Los_Angeles` (agbègbè àkókò ìtúnṣe Anthropic) láìka aago host sí.                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_CONCURRENCY` | `3`                         | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Mélòó ni àwọn ìsopọ̀ tí a máa ń gbóná ní àkókò kan ṣoṣo fún ìgbà ìṣiṣẹ́ kọ̀ọ̀kan. A máa ń dí i sí `1`-`10`; iye tí kì í ṣe nọ́mbà máa ń padà sí `3`.                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_MODEL`       | `claude-3-5-haiku-20241022` | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Model tí a máa ń lò fún ìbéèrè ìgbóná-àkọ́kọ́. Yí i padà nìkan tí àìyípadà kò bá sí lórí ètò rẹ; yan model tó rọrùn jù lọ tí ó ṣì ń ṣí ferese náà.                                                        |

### Àwọn Ìpàdé VNC Ìwọlé-Browser àti Àpèjúwe Data-Dir

Chromium+VNC tí a kó sínú container, tí a ń lò fún gbigba ẹ̀rí ìwọlé-browser ní ìbáṣepọ̀ (`/api/vnc-session`), pẹ̀lú àpèjúwe `DATA_DIR` ogún. Gbogbo wọn jẹ́ àṣàyàn — àwọn àìyípadà VNC máa ń tọ́ka sí image `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local` tí a kó sínú rẹ̀, a sì máa ń yí wọn padà nìkan fún image container àṣà, àwọn pọ́ọ̀tì, tàbí àtúnṣe ìgbésí-ayé.

| Àyípadà                               | Àìyípadà àìpẹ̀yà                                                          | Fáìlì Orísun                                | Àpèjúwe                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IMAGE`                 | `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local`                                           | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ààmì àwòrán Docker fún àpótí ìwọlé Chromium+VNC. Kọ `docker/vnc-browser/chromium` tàbí tọ́ka sí àwòrán àṣà kan níbí.                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_DOCKER_BIN`                | `docker`                                                                 | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Bínárì àkókò-ṣiṣe àpótí tí a lò láti gbé àpótí VNC jáde (fún àpẹẹrẹ, ṣètò sí `podman`).                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_VNC_PORT`    | `3000`                                                                   | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Pọ́ọ̀tì VNC/noVNC tí a ṣí síta nínú àpótí náà.                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_CDP_PORT`    | `9223`                                                                   | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Pọ́ọ̀tì Chrome DevTools Protocol nínú àpótí náà.                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_PROFILE_DIR` | `/config`                                                                | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ọ̀nà sí àkójọ profaili Chromium nínú àpótí náà.                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_PROFILE_DIR`           | `$HOME/.omniroute/browser-login-profiles`                                | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Àkójọ agbàlejò tí ó ń tọ́jú àwọn profaili ìwọlé aṣàwákiri tí a fipamọ́.                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IDLE_MS`               | `600000` (10 min)                                                        | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Àkókò ìdúró (ms) kí ìgbà VNC tí kò ṣiṣẹ́ lè parẹ́.                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_MS`                | `1800000` (30 min)                                                       | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ààlà líle (ms) lórí àkókò ìgbésí-ayé ìgbà VNC kan ṣoṣo.                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_SESSIONS`          | `4`                                                                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Iye tó pọ̀ jù lọ ti àwọn ìgbà VNC tó lè ṣiṣẹ́ papọ̀.                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_READY_MS`              | `45000`                                                                  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Àkókò ìdákẹ́ (ms) tí a ń dúró de aṣàwákiri inú àpótí náà kí ó lè ṣetán fún CDP.                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_HARVEST_MS`            | `20000`                                                                  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Àkókò ìdákẹ́ (ms) fún kíkó ìgbà/àwọn kúkì tí a gbà sílẹ̀ lẹ́yìn tí ìwọlé bá parí.                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CHROMIUM_ARGS`         | `--remote-debugging-port=9222 --no-first-run --no-default-browser-check` | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Àwọn àsíyọ àṣẹ-lílò àfikún tí a ń fi ránṣẹ́ sí Chromium inú àpótí náà.                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_NETWORK`               | `omniroute-vnc-browser-login`                                            | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Nẹ́tíwọ́ọ̀kì Docker pàtàkì tí àpótí ìwọlé VNC darapọ̀ mọ́ (#12571) dípò bridge àìpẹ̀yà, kí àwọn àpótí arákùnrin má bàa lè dé ibi pọ́ọ̀tì afárá CDP rẹ̀.                                                             |
| `VIBEPROXY_DATA_DIR`                  | _(unset)_                                                                | `open-sse/services/notionThreadSessions.ts` | **Àfúnni àtẹ́lẹwọ́** fún `DATA_DIR`, tí a máa ń ṣàyẹ̀wò lẹ́yìn tí `DATA_DIR` àti `OMNIROUTE_DATA_DIR` bá ti jẹ́ àìṣètò. Ó ń tọ́ka sí kaṣe ìgbà àwọn okun wẹ́ẹ̀bù Notion (`<dir>/notion-web-thread-sessions.json`). |

## 26. Idanwo & E2E ijanu

Ti a lo nipasẹ `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`, `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs`, `scripts/dev/run-ecosystem-tests.mjs`, ati `scripts/build/uninstall.mjs`. Fi gbogbo iye silẹ ni isalẹ aito ni awọn imuṣiṣẹ iṣelọpọ.

| Ayípadà                                | Aiyipada                        | Faili orisun                              | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| -------------------------------------- | ------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_E2E_BOOTSTRAP_MODE`         | `auth`                          | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | E2E bootstrap mode (`auth`, `fresh`, `reuse`) fun Playwright olusare.                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_E2E_PASSWORD`               | ṣubu pada si `INITIAL_PASSWORD` | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Ọrọigbaniwọle abojuto itasi sinu agbegbe Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_LOCAL_HEALTHCHECK`  | `true`                          | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Pa idibo ayẹwo ilera agbegbe lakoko awọn ṣiṣe Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_TOKEN_HEALTHCHECK`  | `true`                          | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Muu OAuth tokini loop ṣayẹwo ilera lakoko awọn idanwo.                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEALTHCHECK_SKIP_PROVIDERS` | _(a ko ṣeto)_                   | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`             | Awọn olupese ti o ya sọtọ komama yọkuro kuro ninu gbigba-mimu isọdọtun ti nṣiṣẹ (fun apẹẹrẹ `codex,openai`). Omiiran ìfọkànsí si piparẹ ni kikun awọn olupese ilera — kukuru-TTL ntọju onitura lakoko ti awọn olupese kasikedi duro ni ifaseyin-nikan. |
| `OMNIROUTE_HIDE_HEALTHCHECK_LOGS`      | `true`                          | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Pa ariwo ilera ṣayẹwo ni Playwright stdout.                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLAYWRIGHT_SKIP_BUILD`      | `0`                             | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Rekọja iṣelọpọ Next.js ki o to bẹrẹ Playwright (iṣapejuwe CI ).                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_UNINSTALL_HOOK`        | `0`                             | `scripts/build/uninstall.mjs`             | Rekọja kio aifi sipo OmniRoute (ti o nlo nipasẹ CI lati jẹ ki `node_modules` wa titi).                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `ECOSYSTEM_SERVER_WAIT_MS`             | `180000`                        | `scripts/dev/run-ecosystem-tests.mjs`     | Akoko duro (ms) fun olupin lati ni ilera ṣaaju ṣiṣe awọn idanwo ecosystem/protocol.                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_URL`                   | `http://127.0.0.1:20128/login`  | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | URL ẹfin ẹfin Electron nireti ohun elo ti a kojọpọ lati sin.                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `45000`                         | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Lapapọ akoko ipari (ms) ṣaaju ki ijanu ẹfin to lọ silẹ.                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_SETTLE_MS`             | `2000`                          | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Ṣeto ferese (ms) lẹhin awọn ẹru oju-iwe naa.                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_APP_EXECUTABLE`        | _(laifọwọyi)_                   | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Ọna ti o han gbangba si ṣiṣiṣẹ Electron ti o ṣajọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_DATA_DIR`              | _(tmpdir)_                      | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Itọsọna data fun ṣiṣe ẹfin Electron.                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_KEEP_DATA`             | `0`                             | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Ṣeto `1` lati tọju ilana data ẹfin lẹhin ṣiṣe.                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_STREAM_LOGS`           | `0`                             | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Ṣeto `1` lati san awọn akọọlẹ Electron si stdout lakoko ṣiṣe.                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_COLD_RESTART`          | `0`                             | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | # 7592: tun bẹrẹ lodi si dir data kanna ki o sọ ifilọlẹ keji yan awakọ SQLite abinibi.                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CLI_DEVIN_BIN`                        | _(Ayẹwo PATH )_                 | `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts`         | Yipada si ọna alakomeji Devin CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

### Opopona onitumọ Docs

Ti a lo nipasẹ `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` (aṣẹ `npm run i18n:run` ). Gbogbo awọn oniyipada mẹfa ko ṣeto nipasẹ aiyipada — ṣeto wọn ni `.env` nikan lori awọn ẹrọ ti o yẹ ki o ni anfani lati ṣiṣẹ onitumọ docs.

| Ayípadà                                    | Aiyipada      | Faili orisun                              | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ------------------------------------------ | ------------- | ----------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_API_URL`            | _(a ko ṣeto)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | Ṣii-ibaramu URL ipilẹ AI fun ẹhin itumọ.                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_API_KEY`            | _(a ko ṣeto)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | Àmi olùrùsókè fún ìpìlẹ̀ ìtumọ̀ (kò wọlé láé).                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_MODEL`              | _(a ko ṣeto)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | Awoṣe id, fun apẹẹrẹ `gpt-4o-mini` tabi `cx/gpt-5.4-mini`.                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_FALLBACK_MODELS`    | _(a ko ṣeto)_ | `scripts/i18n/lib/translate-backend.mjs`  | Ẹwọn awoṣe ti o ya sọtọ komama iyan gbiyanju ni ibere nigbati awoṣe akọkọ ba kuna (ipin 429, 401/402, 5xx, akoko ipari, nẹtiwọki); ibeere ti ko dara (400/413/422) ko tun gbiyanju ni ibomiiran. |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_TIMEOUT_MS`         | `60000`       | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | Ibere-akoko akoko-ibere ni awọn iṣẹju-aaya.                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_REASONING_EFFORT`   | _(a ko ṣeto)_ | `scripts/i18n/lib/translate-backend.mjs`  | Iṣakoso ironu iyan, firanṣẹ bi-si ẹhin nigba ti ṣeto.                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_CONCURRENCY`        | `4`           | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | Awọn ibeere itumọ ti o jọra nigbati o nṣiṣẹ lori ọpọlọpọ awọn faili / agbegbe.                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_ENGINE`             | `llm`         | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | `llm` nlo ẹhin oke; `google` tumọ nipasẹ ẹrọ Google -ailewu ati pe ko nilo ọkan ninu awọn oniyipada LLM.                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_GOOGLE_FALLBACK`    | `1`           | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | Pẹlu ẹrọ `llm`, chunk kan ti awoṣe ko le gbejade (oluṣọ ijade kuna lori gbogbo igbiyanju, tabi aṣiṣe awoṣe) jẹ itumọ nipasẹ Google dipo ikuna agbegbe naa. `0` mu u.                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_GOOGLE_API_KEY`     | _(a ko ṣeto)_ | `scripts/i18n/lib/google-translate.mjs`   | Itumọ awọsanma Itumọ v2 bọtini. Unset nlo aaye ipari wẹẹbu ti ko ni bọtini (opin-opin, itanran fun awọn igbi lẹẹkọọkan).                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_GOOGLE_CONCURRENCY` | `6`           | `scripts/i18n/lib/google-translate.mjs`   | Awọn ibeere Google ti o jọra ti o pin nipasẹ gbogbo agbegbe ti ṣiṣe kan.                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_GOOGLE_TIMEOUT_MS`  | `30000`       | `scripts/i18n/lib/google-translate.mjs`   | Akoko akoko-ibere fun ẹnjini Google, ni milliseconds.                                                                                                                                            |
| `UI_KIT_READ_TOKEN`                        | _(a ko ṣeto)_ | `scripts/check/check-ui-kit-upstream.mjs` | Àmi kika-nikan fun ikọkọ ohun elo UI kit repo, ti o nlo nipasẹ `npm run check:ui-kit-upstream` ati iṣisẹ iṣẹ-ọsẹ-ọsẹ "UI Kit upstream watch". Ni agbegbe ayẹwo naa nlo awọn iwe-ẹri git rẹ.      |

## 27. Ifunni Radar (Alejo ti ara ẹni)

Iyan add-on gated nipasẹ RADAR_ENABLED ẹya Flag (aiyipada pa — a ẹya-ara Flag toggled nipasẹ Settings/DB, ko env var; wo [docs/frameworks/RADAR.md](../frameworks/RADAR.md#flag-radar_enabled-default-off) ). Awọn oniyipada mẹrin akọkọ ti o wa ni isalẹ jẹ awọn ifasilẹ iyan fun ifunni ti ara ẹni ti gbalejo tabi orita ati awọn ṣiṣan bọtini alatilẹyin. Karun, `RADAR_ADMIN_URL`, jẹ ọna asopọ aifọwọyi lọtọ lọtọ si igbimọ awọn iṣẹ ikọkọ ti eni. Wo [docs/frameworks/RADAR.md](../frameworks/RADAR.md) fun doc module ni kikun ati [imuṣiṣẹ ipari-si-opin ati ilana iṣeto-itọnisọna](../frameworks/RADAR.md#end-to-end-activation-and-guided-setup).

Oluka ikede Home/Changelog jeneriki ko ni tunto nipasẹ oniyipada ayika ati pe ko dale lori asia ẹya RADAR_ENABLED. O ka ibi ipamọ gbogbo eniyan `news.json` URL ti a kede ni `src/shared/utils/releaseNotes.ts` nipasẹ GET nikan; IDs yiyọ kuro ni ibi ipamọ agbegbe aṣawakiri.

| Ayípadà                       | Aiyipada                                     | Faili orisun                                                 | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                                           |
| ----------------------------- | -------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `RADAR_FEED_URL`              | `https://radar.omniroute.online`             | `src/lib/radar/{sync,referralsSync,offersSync,intelSync}.ts` | Ipilẹ URL ti o pin nipasẹ katalogi ti a fowo si lọtọ, awọn itọkasi, awọn ifunni alatilẹyin, ati awọn ifunni Intel. Pari lati tọka si iṣẹ ti o gbalejo tabi orita. |
| `RADAR_FEED_PUBKEY`           | _(bọtini aiyipada ti a pin) _                | `src/lib/radar/pinnedKeys.ts`                                | Ed25519 bọtini ita gbangba (base64-DER SPKI tabi PEM) ti a lo lati jẹrisi awọn ibuwọlu ifunni lati inu kikọ sii aṣa.                                              |
| `RADAR_CONTRIBUTOR_CLAIM_URL` | `https://radar.omniroute.online/auth/github` | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | URL bọtini dasibodu "Mo jẹ oluranlọwọ" ṣi (ṣàn sisan bọtini alatilẹyin GitHub OAuth ).                                                                            |
| `RADAR_SUPPORTER_PLANS_URL`   | `https://radar.omniroute.online/planos`      | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | URL bọtini dasibodu "Ṣe atilẹyin iṣẹ akanṣe" ṣi (oju-iwe payment/plans ).                                                                                         |
| `RADAR_ADMIN_URL`             | _(a ko ṣeto)_                                | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | Olohun-nikan iṣiṣẹ ikọkọ-panel ọna asopọ. HTTPS nilo ayafi fun HTTP loopback SSH siwaju; aito tabi awọn iye aiṣedeede ko ṣẹda nkan lilọ kiri.                     |

## Ayẹwo: Awọn iyipada ti yọ kuro / ku

Awọn oniyipada atẹle han ni awọn ẹya ti tẹlẹ ti `.env.example` ṣugbọn wọn ni** ko si asiko isise to jo** ninu awọn ti isiyi codebase. Wọn ti yọ kuro:

| Ayípadà                                                                                                                                                                         | Idi                                                                                                                                        |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `STORAGE_DRIVER=sqlite`                                                                                                                                                         | Maṣe ka nipasẹ faili orisun eyikeyi. SQLite jẹ awakọ ti o ni atilẹyin — ko si yiyan ti o nilo.                                             |
| `INSTANCE_NAME=omniroute`                                                                                                                                                       | Wa ninu awọn awoṣe docs/env atijọ ṣugbọn a ko lo ni akoko asiko. Le pada ni ẹya-ara olona-apeere ojo iwaju.                                |
| `SQLITE_MAX_SIZE_MB=2048`                                                                                                                                                       | Ko ṣe itọkasi ni koodu orisun. Iwọn aaye data ko ni opin lainidi.                                                                          |
| `SQLITE_CLEAN_LEGACY_FILES=true`                                                                                                                                                | Ko ṣe itọkasi ni koodu orisun. O ṣeese ki afọmọ-julọ yọkuro.                                                                               |
| `CLI_ROO_BIN`                                                                                                                                                                   | Ko forukọsilẹ ni `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`.                                                                                      |
| `CLI_KIMI_CODING_BIN`                                                                                                                                                           | Ko forukọsilẹ ni `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts` ( Coding Kimi nlo OAuth, kii ṣe alakomeji CLI kan).                                   |
| `IFLOW_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` / `IFLOW_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                                                                                                                           | Ko ṣe itọkasi nibikibi ni koodu orisun.                                                                                                    |
| `CEREBRAS_API_KEY` / `COHERE_API_KEY` / `FIREWORKS_API_KEY` / `GROQ_API_KEY` / `MISTRAL_API_KEY` / `NEBIUS_API_KEY` / `PERPLEXITY_API_KEY` / `TOGETHER_API_KEY` / `XAI_API_KEY` | Ti yọ kuro ni v3.8.0. Akoko asiko ko ka awọn iwe-ẹri env vars — wọnyi wa lati Dashboard / `data/provider-credentials.json` / DB ti paroko. |
| `CURSOR_PROTOBUF_DEBUG`                                                                                                                                                         | Ti yọ kuro ni v3.8.0. oluṣe Cursor `CURSOR_DEBUG` / `CURSOR_STREAM_DEBUG` (wo §22).                                                        |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KIRO`                                                                                                                                                               | Ti yọ kuro ni v3.8.0. Kiro wa ni `CLI_COMPAT_OMITTED_PROVIDER_IDS` — toggle rẹ ko ni ipa.                                                  |
| `QIANFAN_API_KEY`                                                                                                                                                               | Yọ kuro lẹgbẹẹ olupese miiran ti ko lo awọn bọtini bọtini API ni v3.8.0.                                                                   |

### Aiyipada Iye Awọn atunṣe

| Ayípadà                   | Atijo `.env.example` Iye | Iyipada koodu gangan | Ti o wa titi                                        |
| ------------------------- | ------------------------ | -------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- |
| `APP_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`  | `90`                     | `7`                  | ✅ Kuro sinilona iye; `7` ti ṣe akọsilẹ bi aiyipada |
| `CALL_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS` | `90`                     | `7`                  | ✅ Kuro sinilona iye; `7` ti ṣe akọsilẹ bi aiyipada |

### atunto atunto OpenCode (ad-hoc tooling)

Ti a lo nipasẹ `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` lati tun `opencode.json` kan ṣe pẹlu awọn iye `limit.context` deede ati awọn iye `limit.output` ti o fa lati apẹẹrẹ OmniRoute nṣiṣẹ. Ko si ọkan ninu iwọnyi ti a beere fun ṣiṣe deede — iwe afọwọkọ jẹ irinṣẹ oluṣe idagbasoke nikan.

| Ayípadà            | Aiyipada                 | Faili orisun                              | Apejuwe                                                                                                                       |
| ------------------ | ------------------------ | ----------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_URL`    | `http://localhost:20128` | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | Ipilẹ URL ti apẹẹrẹ OmniRoute si ibeere fun `/v1/models`.                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_KEY`    | _(a ko ṣeto)_            | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | bọtini API lati jẹrisi lodi si aaye ipari `/v1/models` OmniRoute. Ṣubu pada si `OPENCODE_API_KEY` nigbati a ko ṣeto.          |
| `OPENCODE_API_KEY` | _(a ko ṣeto)_            | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | Kọ́kọ́rọ́ API ara koodu Ṣii (`sk-...`) ti a kọ sinu `opencode.json` ti a tun ṣe. Ṣubu pada si `OMNIROUTE_KEY` nigbati a ko ṣeto. |

### Funmorawon aisinipo-eval ijanu (irinṣẹ ad-hoc)

Ti a lo nipasẹ `scripts/compression-eval/index.ts`, igbelewọn funmorawon aisinipo CLI. Ko nilo fun iṣẹ ṣiṣe deede — ohun elo oluṣe idagbasoke nikan.

| Ayípadà                      | Aiyipada    | Faili orisun                        | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                                           |
| ---------------------------- | ----------- | ----------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_EVAL_CREDENTIALS` | `{}` (sofo) | `scripts/compression-eval/index.ts` | Awọn iwe eri JSON onišẹ ti pese fun olupese ti a ṣe adaṣe nipasẹ titẹkuro-eval CLI aisinipo (ti a ṣe itupalẹ pẹlu `JSON.parse`). Jẹ ki a ko ṣeto fun ṣiṣe gbigbẹ. |

### VNC Browser Awọn akoko

Ti a lo nipasẹ `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts` lati tunto awọn akoko Chromium alailori-orisun Docker fun awọn olupese ẹrọ aṣawakiri. Gbogbo awọn aiyipada — iyan han ni isalẹ.

| Ayípadà                               | Aiyipada                       | Faili orisun                                | Apejuwe                                                                    |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_DOCKER_BIN`                | `docker`                       | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ona si alakomeji Docker ti a lo lati spawn awọn apoti VNC.                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IMAGE`                 | `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local` | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | aworan Docker fun apoti Chromium VNC.                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CHROMIUM_ARGS`         | _(awọn asia ti a ṣe sinu)_     | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Afikun Chromium CLI args kọja si ẹrọ aṣawakiri inu apoti naa.              |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_VNC_PORT`    | `3000`                         | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | VNC ibudo inu awọn eiyan.                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_CDP_PORT`    | `9223`                         | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Chrome DevTools Protocol ibudo inu awọn eiyan.                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_PROFILE_DIR` | `/config`                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ilana profaili inu eiyan naa.                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_PROFILE_DIR`           | _(a ko ṣeto)_                  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Itọsọna ẹgbẹ-ogun fun awọn profaili aṣawakiri ti o tẹpẹlẹ.                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IDLE_MS`               | `600000`                       | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Aago aiṣiṣẹ (ms) ṣaaju ikore igba VNC kan.                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_MS`                | `1800000`                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Iye akoko ti o pọju (ms).                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_SESSIONS`          | `4`                            | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | O pọju awọn akoko VNC nigbakanna.                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_READY_MS`              | `45000`                        | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Aago imurasilẹ ti aṣawakiri (ms).                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_HARVEST_MS`            | `20000`                        | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | akoko asiko Harvest/cleanup (ms).                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_NETWORK`               | `omniroute-vnc-browser-login`  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Nẹtiwọọki Docker ti a ti sọtọ ti o darapọ mọ (# 12571), pa afara aiyipada. |
| `VIBEPROXY_DATA_DIR`                  | _(a ko ṣeto)_                  | `open-sse/services/notionThreadSessions.ts` | Itọsọna fun itẹramọṣẹ o tẹle igba Notion.                                  |

### Ti abẹnu iṣẹ auth

| Ayípadà                                 | Aiyipada | Apejuwe                                                                                 |
| --------------------------------------- | -------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_INTERNAL_SERVICE_TOKEN`      | –        | Àmi inline fun iṣakoso-ofurufu iṣẹ-si-iṣẹ ìfàṣẹsí.                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_INTERNAL_SERVICE_TOKEN_FILE` | –        | Ọna si faili ti o ni ami iṣẹ inu inu (ayanfẹ ninu awọn apoti; dojukọ oniyipada inline). |

### OpenRouter olupese awọn iṣiro

| Ayípadà                             | Aiyipada  | Apejuwe                                                                                  |
| ----------------------------------- | --------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OPENROUTER_PROVIDER_STATS_ENABLED` | `true`    | Ṣeto si `false` lati foju gbigba OpenRouter fun awọn iṣiro olupese fun imudara katalogi. |
| `OPENROUTER_PROVIDER_STATS_TTL_MS`  | `3600000` | Kaṣe TTL (ms) fun awọn iṣiro olupese OpenRouter ti o mu.                                 |

| Ayípadà                     | Aiyipada    | Apejuwe                                                 |

### ChatGPT Web (Codex)

Globale Defaults für den headless Browser und den ausgehenden Ọpa-Tunnel. Im Dasibodu gesetzte Asopọ-Werte haben Vorrang.

| Ayípadà                                 | Aiyipada                       | Faili orisun                                                               | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| --------------------------------------- | ------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CHROME_PATH`         | _(Ṣawari-laifọwọyi)_           | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Expliziter Chrome-/Chromium-Pfad für npm-, systemd-und PM2-Betrieb.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CHROME_PATH`                           | _(Ṣawari-laifọwọyi)_           | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Gemeinsamer Fallback fun einen expliziten Chrome-/Chromium-Pfad.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CDP_URL`             | _(a ko ṣeto)_                  | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Ti abẹnu CDP-Endpunkt; Docker verwendet den Sidecar auf Port `9223`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_USE_CDP_PROFILE`     | _(kuro — kuro)_                | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/persistent-cdp-context.ts`              | Ṣeto si `1` (pẹlu atunto aaye ipari CDP kan) lati tun lo iṣakoso ita, profaili aṣawakiri ti a wọle si oju-iwe ChatGPT ti o wa dipo ipo ti o ya sọtọ fun titan; tun fi awọn taabu ChatGPT nigbakanna ni 1. Paa nipa aiyipada. Wo [profaili CDP o tẹsiwaju](../guides/CHATGPT-WEB-PERSISTENT-CDP.md). |
| `CDP_PROXY_TOKEN`                       | _(a ko ṣeto)_                  | `docker/chatgpt-web-codex-browser/cdp-proxy.mjs`                           | Wenn gesetzt, muss jede Anfrage an den CDP-Proxy-Sidecar diesen Wert im Header `X-Omni-Cdp-Token` mitschicken (#13679). Ohne Wert leitet der Proxy unauthentifiziert weiter — dann schützt nur die Netzisolierung des Compose-Netzes `chatgpt-web-codex-net`. Erzeugen mit `openssl rand -hex 32`.  |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_TUNNEL_ID`           | _(a ko ṣeto)_                  | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Globale OpenAI-Tunnel-ID fun lilo Codex-Tool-Runden.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_RUNTIME_KEY`         | _(a ko ṣeto)_                  | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Globaler Tunnel Runtime-Kọtini; niemals ni Logs ausgeben.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CONNECTOR_NAME`      | `OmniRoute Codex v2`           | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Exakter Name des neu erstellten ChatGPT-Aṣa-Connectors für die MCP-Brücke.                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_HOME`                | `<DATA_DIR>/chatgpt-web-codex` | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Dediziertes Verzeichnis fun Browser-, Alagbata- ati Tunnelzustand.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BROWSER_DIAGNOSTICS` | `0`                            | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/adapters/chatgpt-web/browser-worker.ts` | Jẹ `1` Browser-Diagnosebilder ati jedem Checkpoint erfasst.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_LAUNCHER`            | _(a ko ṣeto)_                  | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Optionaler absoluter Pfad zu einem dauerhaften jiju-alakomeji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BUN`                 | _(Ṣawari-laifọwọyi)_           | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Optionaler absoluter Pfad zum Bun-Runtime- Alakomeji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CODEX_WEB_GPT_BUN`                     | _(a ko ṣeto)_                  | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Legacy-Fallback für `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BUN`; neue Setups verwenden den kanonischen Namen.                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

## OmniConductor Bridge

Onibara SSE ti o pẹ ti o ṣe afihan awọn iṣẹ-ṣiṣe ibudo OmniConductor sinu agbegbe A2A TaskManager (`src/lib/conductor/`). Jade-ni — Afara nikan bẹrẹ nigbati `CONDUCTOR_HUB_URL` ti ṣeto. Ẹgbẹ olupin nikan: ami ibudo ko gbọdọ de ẹrọ aṣawakiri naa rara.

| Ayípadà                        | Aiyipada                | Faili orisun                     | Apejuwe                                                                                                              |
| ------------------------------ | ----------------------- | -------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CONDUCTOR_HUB_URL`            | _(sofo)_                | `src/lib/conductor/boot.ts`      | Ipilẹ URL ti ibudo OmniConductor (fun apẹẹrẹ `http://127.0.0.1:7910`). Unset = Afara alaabo.                         |
| `CONDUCTOR_HUB_TOKEN`          | _(sofo)_                | `src/lib/conductor/boot.ts`      | Ijẹrisi ibudo fun ifunni SSE — njade `spokesperson` -kind ẹlẹgbẹ lori ibudo (`POST /v1/peers`, abojuto).             |
| `CONDUCTOR_ORCHESTRATOR_TOKEN` | _(sofo)_                | `src/lib/conductor/hubProxy.ts`  | Ijẹrisi fun inbound A2A→ hub iṣẹ aṣoju iṣẹ (`POST /v1/tasks`); ṣubu pada si `CONDUCTOR_HUB_TOKEN` nigbati a ko ṣeto. |
| `CONDUCTOR_SPOKESPERSON_URL`   | `http://127.0.0.1:7920` | `src/lib/conductor/faroProxy.ts` | Ipilẹ URL ti iṣẹ agbẹnusọ (Faro) lẹhin aṣoju iwiregbe dasibodu (`/api/conductor/ask`).                               |

### Iṣeto-kiota-mọ

Ti a lo nipasẹ `open-sse/services/combo.ts` ati `src/lib/quota/quotaScheduler.ts` fun awọn sọwedowo-isuna-ibeere ami-tẹlẹ. Jade-ni ihuwasi afisona aiyipada — ko yipada nigbati aito.

| Ayípadà                            | Aiyipada | Faili orisun                                | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ---------------------------------- | -------- | ------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_AWARE_ROUTING`    | `0`      | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`                | Nigba ti `1`, foo awọn asopọ ti isuna aami-window kọọkan (`rateLimitOverrides.tpm`, tabili `provider_quota_state`) ko le ni idiyele idiyele ibeere ṣaaju fifiranṣẹ. Ikuna-ṣii nigbati ko si iṣeto isuna.                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_ESTIMATOR_CALIBRATION`  | _(lori)_ | `open-sse/services/estimatorCalibration.ts` | Isọdiwọn esi ti iṣiro ami-iṣọ ọrọ-ọrọ (#14931): fun (olupese, awoṣe, awọn irinṣẹ ti o wa) EMA ti `usage.prompt_tokens` / iṣiro, dimọ si 0.15–2.0, ni iranti nikan. `off`, `0` tabi `false` mu gbigbasilẹ ati ohun elo ṣiṣẹ. |
| `GROK_SUBSCRIPTION_IMAGES_ENABLED` | `false`  | `open-sse/config/imageRegistry.ts`          | Ṣe iforukọsilẹ `xai-oauth` (`xao`) ati awọn ọna aworan `grok-cli`. Dasibodu ẹya asia ti kanna orukọ; yi danu DB bori lori iye yi.                                                                                           |

## Awọn ayẹwo imuṣiṣẹpọ awoṣe

| Ayípadà                        | Aiyipada | Faili orisun                                 | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ------------------------------ | -------- | -------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_MODEL_SYNC_TIMINGS` | `0`      | `src/lib/providerModels/modelSyncTimings.ts` | Ṣeto si `1` lati wọle akoko alakoso, ibeere ti ipilẹṣẹ ID ati awọn abajade success/error alakoso lakoko imuṣiṣẹpọ awoṣe. Alaabo fun gbogbo awọn iye miiran. Awọn akọọlẹ fi ibeere silẹ, akọọlẹ, katalogi ati awọn ẹru isanwo aṣiṣe. |

## Ọ̀rò̩ṣẹ́ ìtúpalẹ̀ media OpenCode fún àdúgbò

`Used by` `scripts/cli/omniroute-media-mcp.mjs`. Ṣètò àwọn wọ̀nyí nínú àyíká iná iṣẹ́ ọ̀rò̩ṣẹ́ àdúgbò.

| Àyípadà                    | Àìpẹ̀yà                                                                              | Àlàyé                                                                                                 |
| -------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_MEDIA_CONFIG`   | `$XDG_CONFIG_HOME/opencode/opencode.json` (tàbí `~/.config/opencode/opencode.json`) | Iṣètò OpenCode tí ọ̀rò̩ṣẹ́ media àti ohun èlò fifi sori ẹrọ ń kà.                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_MEDIA_PROVIDER` | `omniroute`                                                                         | Àkọsílẹ̀ olùpèsè tí ọ̀rò̩ṣẹ́ media ń kà láti inú iṣètò OpenCode.                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_MEDIA_MODEL`    | `auto`                                                                              | Àwòṣe tí ọ̀rò̩ṣẹ́ media ń fi ránṣẹ́. Olùpèsè rẹ̀ gbọ́dọ̀ ṣe àtìlẹ́yìn fún àwọn irú media tí a béèrè.          |
| `OMNIROUTE_MEDIA_API_KEY`  | Bọtìnì API olùpèsè tí a ṣètò tàbí ìtọ́kasí àyíká rẹ̀                                  | Ìyípadà ẹ̀rí ìdánimọ̀ tí ó jẹ́ àṣàyàn. Pa á mọ́ nínú àyíká iná iṣẹ́ àdúgbò; má ṣe fi í sínú commit láéláé. |
